{"Write":"C:\\Users\\Admin\\.claude\\plans\\are-there-alternative-options-buzzing-gizmo.md: # Prescripts Storage & Architecture Plan: Cloud Storage & Obsidian Integration\n\n## Context & Objectives\nThe Manager has chosen the **Hybrid CQRS** architecture pattern and asked a vital strategic question:\n*Where should the database live so that the Manager doesn't have to keep a home PC running 24/7 as a server, while frontends/backends can be hosted on serverless platforms like Vercel and accessed across devices from Obsidian and the 4AM ESP32?*\n\nSpecifically, the Manager proposed using **Notion** as the cloud database.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Evaluating Notion as the Cloud Database\n\n### How Notion Would Work in this Architecture:\n1. **Authoring / CMS Layer**: Quotes live inside a Notion Database. The Manager can edit, add, or tag quotes from any phone, laptop, or browser using Notion's native UI.\n2. **Vercel Backend**: A serverless function on Vercel acts as the bridge.\n   - It queries Notion via the Notion REST API (`@notionhq/client`).\n   - It serves `GET /api/today` for the ESP32 OLED at 4AM.\n3. **Obsidian Integration**:\n   - Instead of Obsidian calling the slow Notion API on every quote lookup (which would violate $O(1)$ and fail offline), a periodic or on-demand sync downloads a clean `quotes_map.json` into the Obsidian vault.\n   - Obsidian recites quotes in $O(1)$ constant time from the local JSON cache.\n\n### Notion Trade-Off Analysis:\n| Dimension | Notion Reality | Impact |\n|---|---|---|\n| **Authoring UI** | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ (World-class, mobile app, multi-select, relations) | Effortless data entry from anywhere |\n| **Serverless Hosting** | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ (Zero infrastructure to manage) | Free cloud hosting by Notion |\n| **API Latency** | ⭐⭐ (Slow: 300ms–1200ms per request) | Cannot be queried live per-keystroke; requires caching |\n| **Rate Limits** | ⭐⭐⭐ (Strict 3 requests/sec average limit) | Batch syncing must be rate-limited |\n| **Data Extraction** | ⭐⭐⭐ (Heavy nested AST JSON response) | Vercel backend must flatten Notion property objects |\n| **Offline Reliability**| ⭐ (Cloud-only) | Requires local JSON mirror in Obsidian for offline use |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Comparing Notion with Modern Cloud Database Alternatives\n\n| Feature | Notion Database | Turso (Serverless SQLite) | Supabase (Cloud Postgres) | GitHub Repo as DB (JSON) |\n|---|:---:|:---:|:---:|:---:|\n| **Zero 24/7 Home Server** | ✅ Yes (Notion Cloud) | ✅ Yes (Turso Cloud) | ✅ Yes (Supabase Cloud) | ✅ Yes (GitHub Cloud) |\n| **Vercel Native Deploy** | ✅ Yes (REST API) | ✅ Yes (LibSQL SDK, sub-10ms) | ✅ Yes (Supabase SDK) | ✅ Yes (GitHub API) |\n| **Visual Editing GUI** | ⭐⭐⭐⭐⭐ (Native Notion App) | ⭐⭐⭐ (Web Table / DBeaver) | ⭐⭐⭐⭐ (Airtable-like Web UI) | ⭐⭐ (Code editor) |\n| **Obsidian Access** | Via Synced JSON / Plugin | Via Synced JSON / REST | Via Synced JSON / REST | Native Git Sync / Raw URL |\n| **FTS5 / Complex SQL** | Filter queries only | Full SQLite FTS5 | Full Postgres FTS | Client-side search only |\n| **Cost** | Free Notion plan | Free (500 DBs, 9GB storage) | Free tier | Free |\n\n---\n\n## 3. The Recommended Notion + CQRS Cloud Architecture\n\nIf the Manager selects Notion as the cloud store, the architecture operates as a **Cloud CQRS Pipeline**:\n\n```\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│               NOTION DATABASE (Cloud CMS)              │\n│        Author & edit quotes from any phone or PC       │\n└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘\n                            │\n              Notion REST API (Webhooks / Polling)\n                            │\n                            ▼\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│            VERCEL SERVERLESS EDGE GATEWAY              │\n│                                                        │\n│  1. GET /api/today                                     │\n│     → Serves minimal JSON to ESP32 OLED at 4AM         │\n│                                                        │\n│  2. GET /api/sync                                      │\n│     → Compiles Notion DB into O(1) quotes_map.json     │\n│                                                        │\n│  3. POST /api/quotes                                   │\n│     → Programmatic entry from custom Web UI            │\n└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘\n                            │\n               Periodic Fetch / Obsidian Sync\n                            │\n                            ▼\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 OBSIDIAN VAULT (Local)                 │\n│         data/quotes_map.json (O(1) in-memory)          │\n│                                                        │\n│   • Works 100% offline                                 │\n│   • Instant recitation in notes                        │\n│   • Zero Notion rate-limit bottlenecks                 │\n└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Key Decision Point for the Manager\n- **Path A (Notion as Cloud Master)**: Notion is the visual database. Vercel runs the bridge. Obsidian pulls the compiled JSON.\n- **Path B (Turso Serverless SQLite)**: Pure SQLite on the cloud with sub-10ms edge speed, but edited via a custom web app or database GUI instead of Notion.\n- **Path C (Supabase Postgres)**: Built-in spreadsheet-like web UI plus real-time REST APIs on Vercel.\n"}

{"Write":"C:\\Users\\Admin\\.claude\\plans\\are-there-alternative-options-buzzing-gizmo.md: # Prescripts: Reddit Community Research & Cloud Database Evaluation\n\n## Research Focus\nThe Manager's mandate:\n> *\"I just want my SQLite to be accessible from everywhere and that its data will never lost. Option A (Notion) is not important because latency was too much. Option B (Turso) and Option C (Supabase) show more promise. Please do research on Reddit to see what the community says about them in recent times.\"*\n\n---\n\n## 1. Turso (libSQL Serverless SQLite) — Reddit Consensus\n\n### The Highlights:\n1. **Never Sleeps / No Inactivity Pause**:\n   - Turso's free tier **does not pause or sleep** after inactivity. A 4AM request from an ESP32 will succeed instantly even if the database hasn't received traffic in months.\n2. **True Native SQLite**:\n   - Built on `libSQL` (open-source fork of SQLite). Our existing `Entropy/Rose` schema, FTS5 full-text search, and SQLite queries work directly with zero changes.\n3. **Embedded Replicas (The Killer Feature)**:\n   - Turso allows a client (e.g. desktop script, local app, or Node worker) to keep a **local SQLite file** on disk that continuously replicates with the cloud Turso database. You get local read speeds (sub-1ms) and offline resilience, with cloud synchronization.\n4. **Zero Vendor Lock-in & Zero Data Loss**:\n   - Because it is 100% SQLite, running `turso db dump` produces a standard SQLite `.sql` or `.db` file at any time. If you ever want to leave Turso, you can copy the file to any VPS, USB drive, or Google Drive folder.\n5. **Edge Latency**:\n   - Read queries executed over HTTP/HTTPS from Vercel Edge take single-digit milliseconds (~5–15ms).\n\n### Reddit Caveats:\n- Single-writer architecture: All writes route through the primary region, while reads are distributed worldwide. For Prescripts (single-user personal quote system with low write volume), this is completely irrelevant.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Supabase (Managed Postgres) — Reddit Consensus\n\n### The Fatal Flaw for the 4AM Morning Ritual:\n1. **The 7-Day Inactivity Auto-Pause**:\n   - On Supabase's Free Tier, projects are **automatically paused after 7 consecutive days of inactivity**.\n   - When paused, incoming HTTP requests return `503 Service Unavailable`. It does **not** auto-wake on incoming HTTP requests. A developer must manually log into the web dashboard and click \"Restore\", which takes 1–3 minutes.\n   - **The 4AM Failure Scenario**: If you travel, take a break, or don't add quotes for 7 days, your ESP32 wakes up at 4AM to an unresponsive 503 error, breaking your morning writing routine.\n2. **Aggressive Up-Sell**:\n   - Upgrading to un-pausable Pro costs **$25/month** ($300/year), which is disproportionately expensive for a personal quote system.\n3. **Not SQLite**:\n   - It is PostgreSQL. Migrating from SQLite to Postgres requires rewriting SQLite PRAGMAs, FTS5 triggers, and data types.\n\n---\n\n## 3. The \"Data Will Never Be Lost\" Resilience Strategy\n\nTo fulfill the Manager's requirement that **\"its data will never be lost\"**, we implement a 3-layer safety net:\n\n```\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                   TURSO CLOUD SQLITE                   │\n│        Authoritative Edge Database (Accessible 24/7)   │\n└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘\n                            │\n               Continuous Dual-Sync / Dump\n                            │\n            ┌───────────────┴───────────────┐\n            ▼                               ▼\n┌────────────────────────┐      ┌────────────────────────┐\n│ LOCAL DISK D: BACKUP   │      │ GITHUB BACKUP REPO     │\n│ D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\   │      │ Daily / On-Save Commit │\n│ rose_quotes.db (ACID)  │      │ of quotes.db or .sql   │\n└────────────────────────┘      └────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n1. **Primary**: Turso Cloud Database (serves Vercel, ESP32, and remote devices with zero 24/7 server running).\n2. **Local Mirror**: A local replica or backup on `D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\data\\rose_quotes.db` synced to Google Drive.\n3. **Cold Archive**: Automated export/dump committed to your private GitHub repository on save.\n\nEven if Turso were hit by a meteor, your data is preserved in three independent physical locations (Google Drive Local D:, GitHub Cloud, and Turso Edge).\n"}

           Authority Store (rose_quotes.db / libSQL)               │\n│                                                                        │\n│   • Table 'speakers': id (Hex), name, bio                              │\n│   • Table 'quotes': id (Hex), idx, content, context, date,             │\n│                     speaker_ids_json, preference_json,                 │\n│                     backlinks_json, tags_json, sentiment_json          │\n│   • Virtual Table 'quotes_fts': FTS5 full-text index {"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\compile_projections.py: \"\"\"\nEntropy Rose: Deterministic CQRS Projection Compiler.\nCompiles authoritative SQLite tables into O(1) JSON projection files\nfor instant consumption by Obsidian plugins, Web-OS, and edge clients.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sqlite3\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, List\n\nDEFAULT_DB_PATH = r\"d:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\data\\rose_quotes.db\"\nDEFAULT_OUT_DIR = r\"d:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\data\"\n\n\ndef compile_projections(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH, out_dir: str = DEFAULT_OUT_DIR) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Reads authoritative SQLite tables and generates:\n      1. quotes_map.json: { [hex_id]: QuoteObject } (Denormalized with speaker names for O(1) recite)\n      2. taxonomy_index.json: { speakers: [...], tags: [...], sentiments: [...] } (Suggestion pools)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not os.path.exists(db_path):\n        return {\"quotes\": 0, \"speakers\": 0}\n\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n\n    # 1. Fetch all speakers into an O(1) map\n    speakers_map = {}\n    speakers_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, bio FROM speakers ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            spk = {\"id\": r[\"id\"], \"name\": r[\"name\"], \"bio\": r[\"bio\"] or \"\"}\n            speakers_map[r[\"id\"]] = spk\n            speakers_list.append(spk)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        # Table might not exist yet before migration\n        pass\n\n    # 2. Fetch all quotes\n    quotes_map = {}\n    tags_pool = set()\n    sentiments_pool = set()\n\n    cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT id, idx, main_content, context, date,\n               speaker_name, speaker_role, speaker_origin,\n               tags_json, sentiment, preference_json,\n               COALESCE(speaker_ids_json, '[]') as speaker_ids_json,\n               COALESCE(backlinks_json, '[]') as backlinks_json\n        FROM quotes\n        ORDER BY idx ASC;\n    \"\"\")\n\n    for r in cur.fetchall():\n        qid = r[\"id\"]\n\n        # Parse speaker IDs\n        raw_speaker_ids = r[\"speaker_ids_json\"]\n        speaker_ids = json.loads(raw_speaker_ids) if raw_speaker_ids else []\n\n        # If speaker_ids_json is empty, fall back to legacy speaker_name\n        speakers = []\n        if speaker_ids:\n            for sid in speaker_ids:\n                if sid in speakers_map:\n                    speakers.append(speakers_map[sid])\n                else:\n                    speakers.append({\"id\": sid, \"name\": sid, \"bio\": \"\"})\n        elif r[\"speaker_name\"]:\n            speakers.append({\"id\": \"LEGACY\", \"name\": r[\"speaker_name\"], \"bio\": \"\"})\n\n        # Parse preference (response quote hex IDs)\n        raw_pref = r[\"preference_json\"]\n        preference = json.loads(raw_pref) if raw_pref else []\n\n        # Parse backlinks (URLs)\n        raw_backlinks = r[\"backlinks_json\"]\n        backlinks = json.loads(raw_backlinks) if raw_backlinks else []\n\n        # Parse tags\n        raw_tags = r[\"tags_json\"]\n        if raw_tags:\n            try:\n                parsed_tags = json.loads(raw_tags)\n                if isinstance(parsed_tags, list):\n                    tags = parsed_tags\n                elif isinstance(parsed_tags, dict):\n                    # Legacy schema: flatten domains + themes\n                    tags = parsed_tags.get(\"domains\", []) + parsed_tags.get(\"themes\", [])\n                else:\n                    tags = []\n            except Exception:\n                tags = []\n        else:\n            tags = []\n\n        for t in tags:\n            if isinstance(t, str) and t.strip():\n                tags_pool.add(t.strip())\n\n        # Parse sentiment\n        sentiment = r[\"sentiment\"] or \"\"\n        if sentiment:\n            sentiments_pool.add(sentiment.strip())\n\n        # Construct denormalized quote document\n        quotes_map[qid] = {\n            \"id\": qid,\n            \"index\": r[\"idx\"],\n            \"main_content\": r[\"main_content\"],\n            \"context\": r[\"context\"] or \"\",\n            \"date\": r[\"date\"],\n            \"speakers\": speakers,\n            \"preference\": preference,\n            \"backlinks\": backlinks,\n            \"tags\": tags,\n            \"sentiment\": sentiment\n        }\n\n    conn.close()\n\n    # 3. Write quotes_map.json (O(1) Hash Map)\n    quotes_map_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"quotes_map.json\")\n    with open(quotes_map_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(quotes_map, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    # 4. Write taxonomy_index.json (Suggestion Pools)\n    taxonomy = {\n        \"speakers\": speakers_list,\n        \"tags\": sorted(list(tags_pool)),\n        \"sentiments\": sorted(list(sentiments_pool))\n    }\n    taxonomy_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"taxonomy_index.json\")\n    with open(taxonomy_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(taxonomy, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    return {\"quotes\": len(quotes_map), \"speakers\": len(speakers_list)}\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    stats = compile_projections()\n    print(f\"[OK] Compiled projections: {stats['quotes']} quotes, {stats['speakers']} speakers.\")\n"}

        <input type=\"text\" class=\"raw-input-box\" id=\"input-backlink\" placeholder=\"https://...\" oninput=\"updateBacklink(this.value)\">\n            </div>\n            <div>\n    </div>\n              <input type=\"text\" class=\"raw-input-box\" id=\"input-sentiment\" placeholder=\"analytical\" oninput=\"updateField('sentiment', this.value)\">\n            </div>\n class=\"toast-msg\" id=\"toast\">State Updated</div>\n\n  <script>\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // PRESCRIPTS CLIENT CONTROLLER\n    // ==============================================================================\n\n    // Active Quote State\n    let activeQuote = {\n      id: \"\",\n      index: 1,\n      main_content: \"\",\n      context: \"\",\n      date: \"\",\n      source_speaker: [],\n      preference: [],\n      backlink: [],\n      tags: [],\n      sentiment: \"\"\n    };\n\n    // Pools loaded from backend or local cache\n    let availableSpeakers = [\n      { id: \"0047FB356CE65652\", name: \"Faust\", bio: \"Strategic Analytical Intellect\" },\n      { id: \"0047FB356CE62F42\", name: \"Vinyl Angel\", bio: \"Lyricism from Koi no Koe\" },\n      { id: \"0047FB356CE60001\", name: \"The Manager\", bio: \"Visionary & Commander\" },\n      { id: \"0047FB356CE60002\", name: \"Marcus Aurelius\", bio: \"Stoic Emperor & Philosopher\" }\n    ];\n\n    let availableQuotes = {\n      \"0047FB356CE62F42\": {\n        id: \"0047FB356CE62F42\",\n        index: 1,\n        main_content: \"Static hum, thrum—needle drops,\\nSpinning echo, time just stops.\",\n        context: \"Lyricism from 'Koi no Koe' by Vinyl Angel.\",\n        date: \"2026-09-30\",\n        speaker_name: \"Vinyl Angel\"\n      },\n      \"0047FB356CE65652\": {\n        id: \"0047FB356CE65652\",\n        index: 2,\n        main_content: \"The universe is built on a plan, the profound symmetry of which is somehow present in every inner corner of human thought.\",\n        context: \"Reflections on cybernetics and distributed consciousness.\",\n        date: \"2026-09-30\",\n        speaker_name: \"Faust\"\n      }\n    };\n\n    let availableTags = [\"cybernetics\", \"symmetry\", \"entropy\", \"sovereignty\", \"lyricism\", \"memory\", \"music\", \"morning-ritual\", \"stoic\"];\n\n    // 16-Char Hex ID Generator\n    function generateHexId() {\n      const now = Date.now();\n      const rand = Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xFFFF);\n      return (now.toString(16) + rand.toString(16)).toUpperCase().padStart(16, '0').slice(0, 16);\n    }\n\n    // UI Updates\n    function updateField(field, value) {\n      activeQuote[field] = value;\n      if (!activeQuote.id && activeQuote.main_content.trim()) {\n        activeQuote.id = generateHexId();\n      }\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function updateBacklink(val) {\n      activeQuote.backlink = val.trim() ? [val.trim()] : [];\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function renderLiveJSON() {\n      const pre = document.getElementById('live-json-pre');\n      pre.textContent = JSON.stringify(activeQuote, null, 2);\n      document.getElementById('live-index-badge').textContent = `IDX: ${activeQuote.index}`;\n    }\n\n    function fireToast(msg) {\n      const t = document.getElementById('toast');\n      t.textContent = msg;\n      t.classList.add('show');\n      setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2400);\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 2. FLEXIBLE DATE SUGGESTOR\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function handleDateInput(val) {\n      activeQuote.date = val.trim();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('date-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('date-options-list');\n      const q = val.trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();\n      let suggestions = [];\n\n      if (!q) {\n        suggestions = [\n          `${currentYear}`,\n          `${currentYear}-10`,\n          `${currentYear}-10-02`,\n          `2020~${currentYear}`,\n          `2020`\n        ];\n      } else {\n        // Generate contextual suggestions\n        if (/^\\d{1,4}$/.test(q)) {\n          suggestions.push(q);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}-01`);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}-05`);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}~${currentYear}`);\n        } else if (/^\\d{4}-/.test(q)) {\n          suggestions.push(q);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}-01`);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}-15`);\n        } else {\n          suggestions.push(q);\n        }\n      }\n\n      list.innerHTML = suggestions.slice(0, 5).map(s => `\n        <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row\" onclick=\"selectDate('${s}')\">\n          <span style=\"font-weight:600; font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; color:var(--accent);\">📅 ${s}</span>\n        </div>\n      `).join('');\n\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n    }\n\n    function selectDate(val) {\n      document.getElementById('input-date').value = val;\n      activeQuote.date = val;\n      document.getElementById('date-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 3. SPEAKER MULTI-SELECT\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderSpeakerChips() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('speaker-chips');\n      container.innerHTML = activeQuote.source_speaker.map(sid => {\n        const found = availableSpeakers.find(s => s.id === sid);\n        const name = found ? found.name : sid;\n        return `\n          <span class=\"tag-chip\">\n            <span>👤 ${name}</span>\n            <span class=\"tag-chip-remove\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); removeSpeaker('${sid}')\">✕</span>\n          </span>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function filterSpeakerDropdown(q) {\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('speaker-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('speaker-options-list');\n      const createRow = document.getElementById('speaker-create-row');\n      const query = (q || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const matches = availableSpeakers.filter(s => s.name.toLowerCase().includes(query));\n\n      list.innerHTML = matches.map(s => {\n        const isSelected = activeQuote.source_speaker.includes(s.id);\n        return `\n          <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}\" onclick=\"toggleSpeaker('${s.id}')\">\n            <div class=\"opt-label-group\">\n              <span class=\"opt-title\">${s.name} ${isSelected ? '✓' : ''}</span>\n              ${s.bio ? `<span class=\"opt-context\">${s.bio}</span>` : ''}\n            </div>\n            <button class=\"opt-delete-btn\" title=\"Delete from speaker pool\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); deleteSpeakerPool('${s.id}')\">✕</button>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n\n      if (query && !availableSpeakers.some(s => s.name.toLowerCase() === query)) {\n        createRow.style.display = 'flex';\n        document.getElementById('speaker-create-query').textContent = q.trim();\n      } else {\n        createRow.style.display = 'none';\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleSpeaker(id) {\n      if (activeQuote.source_speaker.includes(id)) {\n        activeQuote.source_speaker = activeQuote.source_speaker.filter(s => s !== id);\n      } else {\n        activeQuote.source_speaker.push(id);\n      }\n      renderSpeakerChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      document.getElementById('speaker-input').value = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('speaker-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n    }\n\n    function removeSpeaker(id) {\n      activeQuote.source_speaker = activeQuote.source_speaker.filter(s => s !== id);\n      renderSpeakerChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function createSpeakerOption() {\n      const input = document.getElementById('speaker-input');\n      const name = input.value.trim();\n      if (!name) return;\n\n      const newId = generateHexId();\n      const newSpk = { id: newId, name: name, bio: \"\" };\n      availableSpeakers.push(newSpk);\n      activeQuote.source_speaker.push(newId);\n\n      // Async post to local server if live\n      fetch('/api/speaker', {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify(newSpk)\n      }).catch(() => {});\n\n      input.value = \"\";\n      renderSpeakerChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      document.getElementById('speaker-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      fireToast(\"Created Speaker: \" + name);\n    }\n\n    function handleSpeakerKey(e) {\n      if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        createSpeakerOption();\n      } else if (e.key === 'Backspace' && e.target.value === '') {\n        if (activeQuote.source_speaker.length > 0) {\n          activeQuote.source_speaker.pop();\n          renderSpeakerChips();\n          renderLiveJSON();\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function deleteSpeakerPool(id) {\n      availableSpeakers = availableSpeakers.filter(s => s.id !== id);\n      activeQuote.source_speaker = activeQuote.source_speaker.filter(s => s !== id);\n      renderSpeakerChips();\n      filterSpeakerDropdown(document.getElementById('speaker-input').value);\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      fireToast(\"Deleted speaker from pool\");\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 4. RESPONSE QUOTES BROWSER (PREFERENCE)\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderPrefChips() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('pref-chips');\n      container.innerHTML = activeQuote.preference.map(qid => {\n        const q = availableQuotes[qid];\n        const snippet = q ? q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 36) + '...' : qid.substring(0, 8);\n        return `\n          <span class=\"tag-chip\" title=\"${q ? q.main_content : qid}\">\n            <span>💬 ${snippet}</span>\n            <span class=\"tag-chip-remove\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); removePref('${qid}')\">✕</span>\n          </span>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function filterPrefDropdown(q) {\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('pref-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('pref-options-list');\n      const query = (q || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const matches = Object.values(availableQuotes).filter(quote => {\n        return quote.main_content.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||\n               (quote.context && quote.context.toLowerCase().includes(query)) ||\n               (quote.speaker_name && quote.speaker_name.toLowerCase().includes(query));\n      });\n\n      if (matches.length === 0) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.85rem; padding:0.5rem;\">No matching quotes found.</div>';\n        return;\n      }\n\n      list.innerHTML = matches.map(quote => {\n        const isSelected = activeQuote.preference.includes(quote.id);\n        const firstLine = quote.main_content.split('\\n')[0];\n        return `\n          <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}\" onclick=\"togglePref('${quote.id}')\">\n            <div class=\"opt-label-group\">\n              <span class=\"opt-title\">“${firstLine}” ${isSelected ? '✓' : ''}</span>\n              <span class=\"opt-context\">${quote.speaker_name || 'Author'} · ${quote.date} · ${quote.context || ''}</span>\n            </div>\n            <span style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.7rem; color:var(--text-dim);\">#${quote.id.substring(0,8)}</span>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function togglePref(id) {\n      if (activeQuote.preference.includes(id)) {\n        activeQuote.preference = activeQuote.preference.filter(q => q !== id);\n      } else {\n        activeQuote.preference.push(id);\n      }\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      document.getElementById('pref-input').value = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('pref-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n    }\n\n    function removePref(id) {\n      activeQuote.preference = activeQuote.preference.filter(q => q !== id);\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 5. MULTIPLE-CHOICE TAG POOL\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderTagBoard() {\n      const board = document.getElementById('tag-board');\n      board.innerHTML = availableTags.map(tag => {\n        const isActive = activeQuote.tags.includes(tag);\n        return `\n          <div class=\"tag-choice-pill ${isActive ? 'active' : ''}\" onclick=\"toggleTagChoice('${tag}')\">\n            <span># ${tag}</span>\n            <span class=\"tag-choice-delete\" title=\"Delete from tag pool\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); deleteTagChoice('${tag}')\">✕</span>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n\n      document.getElementById('active-tag-count').textContent = `${activeQuote.tags.length} selected`;\n    }\n\n    function toggleTagChoice(tag) {\n      if (activeQuote.tags.includes(tag)) {\n        activeQuote.tags = activeQuote.tags.filter(t => t !== tag);\n      } else {\n        activeQuote.tags.push(tag);\n      }\n      renderTagBoard();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function addNewTagFromInput() {\n      const input = document.getElementById('input-new-tag');\n      const val = input.value.replace(/#/g, '').trim().toLowerCase();\n      if (!val) return;\n\n      if (!availableTags.includes(val)) {\n        availableTags.push(val);\n      }\n      if (!activeQuote.tags.includes(val)) {\n        activeQuote.tags.push(val);\n      }\n\n      input.value = \"\";\n      renderTagBoard();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      fireToast(\"Added tag: \" + val);\n    }\n\n    function handleNewTagKey(e) {\n      if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        addNewTagFromInput();\n      }\n    }\n\n    function deleteTagChoice(tag) {\n      availableTags = availableTags.filter(t => t !== tag);\n      activeQuote.tags = activeQuote.tags.filter(t => t !== tag);\n      renderTagBoard();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      fireToast(\"Deleted tag from pool: \" + tag);\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // SAVE & EXPORT PIPELINE\n    // ==============================================================================\n    async function saveQuote() {\n      if (!activeQuote.main_content.trim()) {\n 'light' : 'dark';\n      html.setAttribute('data-theme', nxt);\n      localStorage.setItem('prescripts-theme', nxt);\n    }\n\n    function focusInput(id) {\n      document.getElementById(id).focus();\n    }\n\n    // Load initial taxonomy & quotes from local server\n    async function fetchBackendData() {\n      try {\n        const taxRes = await renderSpeakerChips();\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderTagBoard();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    // Global click listener to close dropdowns\n    document.addEventListener('click', -->\n  <div class=\"toast-msg\" id=\"toast\">State Updated</div>\n\n  <script>\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // PRESCRIPTS CLIENT CONTROLLER — UNIFIED SUGGESTOR ENGINE\n    // ==============================================================================\n\n    let activeQuote = {\n      id: \"\",\n      index: 1,\n      main_content: \"\",\n      context: \"\",\n      date: \"\",\n      source_speaker: [],\n      preference: [],\n      backlink: [],\n      tags: [],\n      sentiment: \"\"\n    };\n\n    let availableSpeakers = [\n      { id: \"0047FB356CE65652\", name: \"Faust\", bio: \"Strategic Analytical Intellect\" },\n      { id: \"0047FB356CE62F42\", name: \"Vinyl Angel\", bio: \"Lyricism from Koi no Koe\" },\n      { id: \"0047FB356CE60001\", name: \"The Manager\", bio: \"Visionary & Commander\" },\n      { id: \"0047FB356CE60002\", name: \"Marcus Aurelius\", bio: \"Stoic Emperor & Philosopher\" }\n    ];\n\n    let availableQuotes = {\n      \"0047FB356CE62F42\": {\n        id: \"0047FB356CE62F42\",\n        index: 1,\n        main_content: \"Static hum, thrum—needle drops,\\nSpinning echo, time just stops.\",\n        context: \"Lyricism from 'Koi no Koe' by Vinyl Angel.\",\n        date: \"2026-09-30\",\n        speaker_name: \"Vinyl Angel\"\n      },\n      \"0047FB356CE65652\": {\n        id: \"0047FB356CE65652\",\n        index: 2,\n        main_content: \"The universe is built on a plan, the profound symmetry of which is somehow present in every inner corner of human thought.\",\n        context: \"Reflections on cybernetics and distributed consciousness.\",\n        date: \"2026-09-30\",\n        speaker_name: \"Faust\"\n      }\n    };\n\n    let availableTags = [\"cybernetics\", \"symmetry\", \"entropy\", \"sovereignty\", \"lyricism\", \"memory\", \"music\", \"morning-ritual\", \"stoic\"];\n\n    function generateHexId() {\n      const now = Date.now();\n      const rand = Math.floor(Math.random() * 0xFFFF);\n      return (now.toString(16) + rand.toString(16)).toUpperCase().padStart(16, '0').slice(0, 16);\n    }\n\n    function updateField(field, value) {\n      activeQuote[field] = value;\n      if (!activeQuote.id && activeQuote.main_content.trim()) {\n        activeQuote.id = generateHexId();\n      }\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function updateBacklink(val) {\n      activeQuote.backlink = val.trim() ? [val.trim()] : [];\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function renderLiveJSON() {\n      const pre = document.getElementById('live-json-pre');\n      pre.textContent = JSON.stringify(activeQuote, null, 2);\n      document.getElementById('live-index-badge').textContent = `IDX: ${activeQuote.index}`;\n    }\n\n    function fireToast(msg) {\n      const t = document.getElementById('toast');\n      t.textContent = msg;\n      t.classList.add('show');\n      setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2400);\n    }\n\n    function closeAllDropdowns() {\n      document.querySelectorAll('.notion-dropdown-menu').forEach(dd => dd.classList.remove('open'));\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 2. FLEXIBLE DATE SUGGESTOR\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function handleDateInput(val) {\n      activeQuote.date = val.trim();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('date-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('date-options-list');\n      const q = val.trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();\n      let suggestions = [];\n\n      if (!q) {\n        suggestions = [\n          `${currentYear}`,\n          `${currentYear}-10`,\n          `${currentYear}-10-02`,\n          `2020~${currentYear}`,\n          `2020`\n        ];\n      } else {\n        if (/^\\d{1,4}$/.test(q)) {\n          suggestions.push(q);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}-01`);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}-05`);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}~${currentYear}`);\n        } else if (/^\\d{4}-/.test(q)) {\n          suggestions.push(q);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}-01`);\n          suggestions.push(`${q}-15`);\n        } else {\n          suggestions.push(q);\n        }\n      }\n\n      list.innerHTML = suggestions.slice(0, 6).map(s => `\n        <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row\" onclick=\"selectDate('${s}')\">\n          <div class=\"opt-label-group\">\n            <span class=\"opt-title\" style=\"color:var(--accent); font-family:'JetBrains Mono';\">📅 ${s}</span>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      `).join('');\n    }\n\n    function selectDate(val) {\n      document.getElementById('input-date').value = val;\n      activeQuote.date = val;\n      document.getElementById('date-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function clearDateInput() {\n      document.getElementById('input-date').value = \"\";\n      activeQuote.date = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('date-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function handleDateKey(e) {\n      if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n        document.getElementById('date-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n        document.getElementById('date-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 3. SPEAKER SUGGESTOR (MULTI-CHOICE)\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderSpeakerChips() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('speaker-chips');\n      container.innerHTML = activeQuote.source_speaker.map(sid => {\n        const found = availableSpeakers.find(s => s.id === sid);\n        const name = found ? found.name : sid;\n        return `\n          <span class=\"chip-badge\">\n            <span>👤 ${name}</span>\n            <span class=\"chip-remove\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); removeSpeaker('${sid}')\">✕</span>\n          </span>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function filterSpeakerDropdown(q) {\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('speaker-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('speaker-options-list');\n      const createRow = document.getElementById('speaker-create-row');\n      const query = (q || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const matches = availableSpeakers.filter(s => s.name.toLowerCase().includes(query));\n\n      if (matches.length === 0) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.85rem; padding:0.5rem;\">No matching speakers in pool.</div>';\n      } else {\n        list.innerHTML = matches.map(s => {\n          const isSelected = activeQuote.source_speaker.includes(s.id);\n          return `\n            <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}\" onclick=\"toggleSpeaker('${s.id}')\">\n              <div class=\"opt-label-group\">\n                <div class=\"opt-title-row\">\n                  <span class=\"opt-check\">${isSelected ? '✓' : '+'}</span>\n                  <span class=\"opt-title\">${s.name}</span>\n                </div>\n                ${s.bio ? `<span class=\"opt-context\">${s.bio}</span>` : ''}\n              </div>\n              <button class=\"opt-delete-btn\" title=\"Delete from pool\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); deleteSpeakerPool('${s.id}')\">✕</button>\n            </div>\n          `;\n        }).join('');\n      }\n\n      if (query && !availableSpeakers.some(s => s.name.toLowerCase() === query)) {\n        createRow.style.display = 'flex';\n        document.getElementById('speaker-create-query').textContent = q.trim();\n      } else {\n        createRow.style.display = 'none';\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleSpeaker(id) {\n      if (activeQuote.source_speaker.includes(id)) {\n        activeQuote.source_speaker = activeQuote.source_speaker.filter(s => s !== id);\n      } else {\n        activeQuote.source_speaker.push(id);\n      }\n      renderSpeakerChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      filterSpeakerDropdown(document.getElementById('speaker-input').value);\n    }\n\n    function removeSpeaker(id) {\n      activeQuote.source_speaker = activeQuote.source_speaker.filter(s => s !== id);\n      renderSpeakerChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      filterSpeakerDropdown(document.getElementById('speaker-input').value);\n    }\n\n    function clearSpeakerInput() {\n      document.getElementById('speaker-input').value = \"\";\n      filterSpeakerDropdown(\"\");\n    }\n\n    function createSpeakerOption() {\n      const input = document.getElementById('speaker-input');\n      const name = input.value.trim();\n      if (!name) return;\n\n      const newId = generateHexId();\n      const newSpk = { id: newId, name: name, bio: \"\" };\n      availableSpeakers.push(newSpk);\n      activeQuote.source_speaker.push(newId);\n\n      fetch('/api/speaker', {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify(newSpk)\n      }).catch(() => {});\n\n      input.value = \"\";\n      renderSpeakerChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      filterSpeakerDropdown(\"\");\n      fireToast(\"Created Speaker: \" + name);\n    }\n\n    function handleSpeakerKey(e) {\n      if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n        e.preventDefault();\n        const input = document.getElementById('speaker-input');\n        const q = input.value.trim();\n        if (q) {\n          const match = availableSpeakers.find(s => s.name.toLowerCase() === q.toLowerCase());\n          if (match) {\n            toggleSpeaker(match.id);\n            input.value = \"\";\n          } else {\n            createSpeakerOption();\n          }\n        }\n      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n        document.getElementById('speaker-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      }\n    }\n\n    function deleteSpeakerPool(id) {\n      availableSpeakers = availableSpeakers.filter(s => s.id !== id);\n      activeQuote.source_speaker = activeQuote.source_speaker.filter(s => s !== id);\n      renderSpeakerChips();\n      filterSpeakerDropdown(document.getElementById('speaker-input').value);\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      fireToast(\"Deleted speaker from pool\");\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 4. RESPONSE QUOTES SUGGESTOR (MULTI-CHOICE)\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderPrefChips() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('pref-chips');\n      container.innerHTML = activeQuote.preference.map(qid => {\n        const q = availableQuotes[qid];\n        const snippet = q ? q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 36) + '...' : qid.substring(0, 8);\n        return `\n          <span class=\"chip-badge\" title=\"${q ? q.main_content : qid}\">\n            <span>💬 ${snippet}</span>\n            <span class=\"chip-remove\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); removePref('${qid}')\">✕</span>\n          </span>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function filterPrefDropdown(q) {\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('pref-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('pref-options-list');\n      const query = (q || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const matches = Object.values(availableQuotes).filter(quote => {\n        return quote.main_content.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||\n               (quote.context && quote.context.toLowerCase().includes(query)) ||\n               (quote.speaker_name && quote.speaker_name.toLowerCase().includes(query));\n      });\n\n      if (matches.length === 0) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.85rem; padding:0.5rem;\">No matching quotes found.</div>';\n        return;\n      }\n\n      list.innerHTML = matches.map(quote => {\n        const isSelected = activeQuote.preference.includes(quote.id);\n        const firstLine = quote.main_content.split('\\n')[0];\n        return `\n          <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}\" onclick=\"togglePref('${quote.id}')\">\n            <div class=\"opt-label-group\">\n              <div class=\"opt-title-row\">\n                <span class=\"opt-check\">${isSelected ? '✓' : '+'}</span>\n                <span class=\"opt-title\">“${firstLine}”</span>\n              </div>\n              <span class=\"opt-context\">${quote.speaker_name || 'Author'} · ${quote.date} · ${quote.context || ''}</span>\n            </div>\n            <span style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.7rem; color:var(--text-dim);\">#${quote.id.substring(0,8)}</span>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function togglePref(id) {\n      if (activeQuote.preference.includes(id)) {\n        activeQuote.preference = activeQuote.preference.filter(q => q !== id);\n      } else {\n        activeQuote.preference.push(id);\n      }\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      filterPrefDropdown(document.getElementById('pref-input').value);\n    }\n\n    function removePref(id) {\n      activeQuote.preference = activeQuote.preference.filter(q => q !== id);\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      filterPrefDropdown(document.getElementById('pref-input').value);\n    }\n\n    function clearPrefInput() {\n      document.getElementById('pref-input').value = \"\";\n      filterPrefDropdown(\"\");\n    }\n\n    function handlePrefKey(e) {\n      if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n        document.getElementById('pref-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 5. TAGS SUGGESTOR (MULTI-CHOICE)\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderTagChips() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('tag-chips');\n      container.innerHTML = activeQuote.tags.map(tag => `\n        <span class=\"chip-badge\">\n          <span># ${tag}</span>\n          <span class=\"chip-remove\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); removeTag('${tag}')\">✕</span>\n        </span>\n      `).join('');\n    }\n\n    function filterTagDropdown(q) {\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('tag-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('tag-options-list');\n      const createRow = document.getElementById('tag-create-row');\n      const query = (q || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const matches = availableTags.filter(t => t.toLowerCase().includes(query));\n\n      if (matches.length === 0 && !query) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.85rem; padding:0.5rem;\">No tags in pool.</div>';\n      } else {\n        list.innerHTML = matches.map(tag => {\n          const isSelected = activeQuote.tags.includes(tag);\n          return `\n            <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row ${isSelected ? {"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\compile_projections.py: \"\"\"\nEntropy Rose: Deterministic CQRS Projection Compiler.\nCompiles authoritative SQLite tables into O(1) JSON projection files\nfor instant consumption by Obsidian plugins, Web-OS, and edge clients.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sqlite3\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, List\n\nBASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nDEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\", \"rose_quotes.db\")\nDEFAULT_OUT_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\")\n\n\ndef compile_projections(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH, out_dir: str = DEFAULT_OUT_DIR) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Reads authoritative SQLite tables and generates:\n      1. quotes_map.json: { [hex_id]: QuoteObject } (Denormalized with speaker names for O(1) recite)\n      2. taxonomy_index.json: { speakers: [...], tags: [...], sentiments: [...] } (Suggestion pools)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not os.path.exists(db_path):\n        return {\"quotes\": 0, \"speakers\": 0}\n\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n\n    # 1. Fetch all speakers into an O(1) map\n    speakers_map = {}\n    speakers_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, bio FROM speakers ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            spk = {\"id\": r[\"id\"], \"name\": r[\"name\"], \"bio\": r[\"bio\"] or \"\"}\n            speakers_map[r[\"id\"]] = spk\n            speakers_list.append(spk)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 2. Fetch all quotes using the consolidated 10-column schema\n    quotes_map = {}\n    tags_pool = set()\n    sentiments_pool = set()\n\n    cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n        SELECT id, idx, main_content, context, date,\n               COALESCE(speaker_ids, '[]') as speaker_ids,\n               COALESCE(preference, '[]') as preference,\n               COALESCE(backlinks, '[]') as backlinks,\n               COALESCE(tags, '[]') as tags,\n               COALESCE(sentiment, '') as sentiment\n        FROM quotes\n        ORDER BY idx ASC;\n    \"\"\")\n\n    for r in cur.fetchall():\n        qid = r[\"id\"]\n\n        # Parse speaker IDs\n        speaker_ids = json.loads(r[\"speaker_ids\"]) if r[\"speaker_ids\"] else []\n        speakers = []\n        for sid in speaker_ids:\n            if sid in speakers_map:\n                speakers.append(speakers_map[sid])\n            else:\n                speakers.append({\"id\": sid, \"name\": sid, \"bio\": \"\"})\n\n        # Parse preference\n        preference = json.loads(r[\"preference\"]) if r[\"preference\"] else []\n\n        # Parse backlinks\n        backlinks = json.loads(r[\"backlinks\"]) if r[\"backlinks\"] else []\n\n        # Parse tags\n        raw_tags = json.loads(r[\"tags\"]) if r[\"tags\"] else []\n        tags = raw_tags if isinstance(raw_tags, list) else []\n        for t in tags:\n            if isinstance(t, str) and t.strip():\n                tags_pool.add(t.strip())\n\n        # Parse sentiment\n        sentiment = r[\"sentiment\"] or \"\"\n        if sentiment:\n            sentiments_pool.add(sentiment.strip())\n\n        # Construct denormalized quote document for O(1) recite\n        quotes_map[qid] = {\n            \"id\": qid,\n            \"index\": r[\"idx\"],\n            \"main_content\": r[\"main_content\"],\n            \"context\": r[\"context\"] or \"\",\n            \"date\": r[\"date\"],\n            \"speakers\": speakers,\n            \"preference\": preference,\n            \"backlinks\": backlinks,\n            \"tags\": tags,\n            \"sentiment\": sentiment\n        }\n\n    conn.close()\n\n    # 3. Write quotes_map.json (O(1) Hash Map)\n    quotes_map_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"quotes_map.json\")\n    with open(quotes_map_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(quotes_map, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    # 4. Write taxonomy_index.json (Suggestion Pools)\n    taxonomy = {\n        \"speakers\": speakers_list,\n        \"tags\": sorted(list(tags_pool)),\n        \"sentiments\": sorted(list(sentiments_pool))\n    }\n    taxonomy_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"taxonomy_index.json\")\n    with open(taxonomy_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(taxonomy, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    return {\"quotes\": len(quotes_map), \"speakers\": len(speakers_list)}\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    stats = compile_projections()\n    print(f\"[OK] Compiled projections: {stats['quotes']} quotes, {stats['speakers']} speakers.\")\n"}

identifiers:\n  <4 HEX PREFIX><12 HEX UNIX MILLISECONDS>\nPrefixes:\n  - 0001: Quotes\n  - 0002: Containers (Origin / Franchise / Creator)\n  - 0003: Specifics (Work / Character / Diary / Wisdom)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport time\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nimport json\nimport os\n\nBASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\n\nPREFIX_QUOTE = \"0001\"\nPREFIX_CONTAINER = \"0002\"\nPREFIX_SPECIFIC = \"0003\"\n\n# Canonical fixed IDs for foundational seed entities\nCANONICAL_ID_VINYL_CONTAINER = \"00020001A1088D80\"\nCANONICAL_ID_FAUST_CONTAINER = \"00020001A1088D81\"\nCANONICAL_ID_MANAGER_CONTAINER = \"00020001A1088D82\"\nCANONICAL_ID_MARCUS_CONTAINER = \"00020001A1088D83\"\nCANONICAL_ID_BLAKE_CONTAINER = \"00020001A1088D84\"\nCANONICAL_ID_EVA_CONTAINER = \"00020001A1088D85\"\n\nCANONICAL_ID_KOI_SPECIFIC = \"00030001A1088D80\"\nCANONICAL_ID_FAUST_SPECIFIC = \"00030001A1088D81\"\nCANONICAL_ID_DIARY_SPECIFIC = \"00030001A1088D82\"\nCANONICAL_ID_WISDOM_SPECIFIC = \"00030001A1088D83\"\nCANONICAL_ID_MEDITATIONS_SPECIFIC = \"00030001A1088D84\"\nCANONICAL_ID_INFANT_JOY_SPECIFIC = \"00030001A1088D85\"\nCANONICAL_ID_ASUKA_SPECIFIC = \"00030001A1088D86\"\n\nCANONICAL_ID_VINYL_QUOTE = \"00010001A1088D80\"\nCANONICAL_ID_FAUST_QUOTE = \"00010001A1088D81\"\n\n\ndef generate_hex_id(prefix: str = PREFIX_QUOTE, unix_ms: int = None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generates a 16-character uppercase hex identifier:\n      <4 HEX PREFIX><12 HEX UNIX MILLISECONDS>\n    \"\"\"\n    if unix_ms is None:\n        unix_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)\n    clean_prefix = str(prefix).upper().rjust(4, \"0\")[:4]\n    hex_ts = f\"{unix_ms:012X}\"[-12:]\n    return f\"{clean_prefix}{hex_ts}\"\n\n\ndef parse_hex_id_timestamp(hex_id: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Extracts Unix epoch timestamp (in seconds) directly from the 16-char hex ID.\"\"\"\n    if len(hex_id) < 16:\n        return 0.0\n    unix_ms = int(hex_id[4:], 16)\n    return unix_ms / 1000.0\n\n\ndef create_sample_quotes(deterministic: bool = True):\n    if deterministic:\n        id_vinyl = CANONICAL_ID_VINYL_QUOTE\n        id_faust = CANONICAL_ID_FAUST_QUOTE\n        date_str = \"2026-09-30\"\n    else:\n        now_ms = int(time.time() * 1000)\n        id_vinyl = generate_hex_id(PREFIX_QUOTE, now_ms - 5000)\n        id_faust = generate_hex_id(PREFIX_QUOTE, now_ms)\n        date_str = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n\n    import db\n    db.init_db()\n\n    # 1. Seed Containers (Table 2)\n    core_containers = [\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_VINYL_CONTAINER, \"name\": \"Vinyl Angel\", \"type\": \"persona\", \"notes\": \"Project & Acoustic Fiction\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_FAUST_CONTAINER, \"name\": \"Faust\", \"type\": \"intellect\", \"notes\": \"Strategic Analytical Intellect\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_MANAGER_CONTAINER, \"name\": \"The Manager\", \"type\": \"personal\", \"notes\": \"Visionary & Commander\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_MARCUS_CONTAINER, \"name\": \"Marcus Aurelius\", \"type\": \"author\", \"notes\": \"Stoic Emperor\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_BLAKE_CONTAINER, \"name\": \"William Blake\", \"type\": \"author\", \"notes\": \"Romantic Poet & Painter\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_EVA_CONTAINER, \"name\": \"Neon Genesis Evangelion\", \"type\": \"anime\", \"notes\": \"Studio Gainax\"}\n    ]\n    for c in core_containers:\n        db.upsert_container(c)\n\n    # 2. Seed Specifics (Table 3)\n    core_specifics = [\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_KOI_SPECIFIC, \"name\": \"Koi no Koe\", \"container_id\": CANONICAL_ID_VINYL_CONTAINER, \"category\": \"music\", \"notes\": \"\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_FAUST_SPECIFIC, \"name\": \"System Reflections\", \"container_id\": CANONICAL_ID_FAUST_CONTAINER, \"category\": \"insight\", \"notes\": \"\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_DIARY_SPECIFIC, \"name\": \"Diary\", \"container_id\": CANONICAL_ID_MANAGER_CONTAINER, \"category\": \"diary\", \"notes\": \"Personal daily life reflections\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_WISDOM_SPECIFIC, \"name\": \"Wisdom\", \"container_id\": CANONICAL_ID_MANAGER_CONTAINER, \"category\": \"wisdom\", \"notes\": \"Accumulated personal meta-rules\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_MEDITATIONS_SPECIFIC, \"name\": \"Meditations\", \"container_id\": CANONICAL_ID_MARCUS_CONTAINER, \"category\": \"book\", \"notes\": \"Greek notes to himself\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_INFANT_JOY_SPECIFIC, \"name\": \"Infant Joy\", \"container_id\": CANONICAL_ID_BLAKE_CONTAINER, \"category\": \"poem\", \"notes\": \"Songs of Innocence (1789)\"},\n        {\"id\": CANONICAL_ID_ASUKA_SPECIFIC, \"name\": \"Asuka Langley Soryu\", \"container_id\": CANONICAL_ID_EVA_CONTAINER, \"category\": \"character\", \"notes\": \"Second Child\"}\n    ]\n    for s in core_specifics:\n        db.upsert_specific(s)\n\n    # 3. Seed Quotes (Table 1)\n    quote_1 = {\n        \"id\": id_vinyl,\n        \"index\": 1,\n        \"main_content\": \"Static hum, thrum—needle drops,\\nSpinning echo, time just stops.\\nFamiliar chords fill up the room,\\nWrapped inside this faded tomb.\",\n        \"context\": \"Lyricism from 'Koi no Koe' by Vinyl Angel. The needle touching groove marks the suspended temporal boundary where sound transcends decaying physical media—evoking acoustic memory and temporal suspension.\",\n        \"date\": date_str,\n        \"specific_ids\": [CANONICAL_ID_KOI_SPECIFIC],\n        \"preference\": [],\n        \"backlinks\": [\"https://vinylangel.net/lyrics/koi-no-koe\"],\n        \"tags\": [\"music\", \"lyricism\"],\n        \"sentiment\": \"nostalgic\"\n    }\n\n    quote_2 = {\n        \"id\": id_faust,\n        \"index\": 2,\n        \"main_content\": \"The universe is built on a plan, the profound symmetry of which is somehow present in every inner corner of human thought.\\nWe are not passive observers; we are the system observing itself.\",\n        \"context\": \"Reflections on cybernetics and distributed consciousness.\\nNoted while constructing the Faust Hivemind infrastructure at the nexus of order and entropy.\",\n        \"date\": date_str,\n        \"specific_ids\": [CANONICAL_ID_FAUST_SPECIFIC],\n        \"preference\": [id_vinyl],\n        \"backlinks\": [],\n        \"tags\": [\"wisdom\", \"cybernetics\"],\n        \"sentiment\": \"analytical\"\n    }\n\n    out_dir = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\")\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Primary O(1) Hash-Map Store\n    json_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"quotes.json\")\n    with open(json_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump({id_vinyl: quote_1, id_faust: quote_2}, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    # Append-only event log\n    jsonl_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"quotes.jsonl\")\n    with open(jsonl_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(json.dumps(quote_1, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n        f.write(json.dumps(quote_2, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n\n    db.upsert_quote(quote_1, compile_after=False)\n    db.upsert_quote(quote_2, compile_after=True)\n\n    print(f\"[OK] Seeded Quote #1 (Koi no Koe · Vinyl Angel) [{id_vinyl}]\")\n    print(f\"[OK] Seeded Quote #2 (System Reflections · Faust) [{id_faust}]\")\n    print(f\"[OK] Synchronized SQLite rose_quotes.db and compiled projections\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    create_sample_quotes()\n"}

{"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\compile_projections.py: \"\"\"\nEntropy Rose / Prescripts: Deterministic CQRS Projection Compiler.\nCompiles the pure 3-table relational schema:\n  quotes -> specifics -> containers\ninto O(1) denormalized JSON projection maps for Obsidian and edge clients.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sqlite3\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, List\n\nBASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nDEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\", \"rose_quotes.db\")\nDEFAULT_OUT_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\")\n\n\ndef compile_projections(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH, out_dir: str = DEFAULT_OUT_DIR) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Reads authoritative SQLite tables and generates:\n      1. quotes_map.json: { [hex_id]: QuoteObject } (Denormalized with joined Specifics & Containers for O(1) recite)\n      2. taxonomy_index.json: { containers: [...], specifics: [...], tags: [...], sentiments: [...] }\n    \"\"\"\n    if not os.path.exists(db_path):\n        return {\"quotes\": 0, \"specifics\": 0, \"containers\": 0}\n\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n\n    # 1. Fetch all containers (Table 2)\n    containers_map = {}\n    containers_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, type, notes FROM containers ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            c = {\"id\": r[\"id\"], \"name\": r[\"name\"], \"type\": r[\"type\"] or \"\", \"notes\": r[\"notes\"] or \"\"}\n            containers_map[r[\"id\"]] = c\n            containers_list.append(c)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 2. Fetch all specifics (Table 3) pre-joined with containers\n    specifics_map = {}\n    specifics_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, container_id, category, notes FROM specifics ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            cid = r[\"container_id\"]\n            parent_container = containers_map.get(cid, {\"id\": cid, \"name\": cid, \"type\": \"\"})\n            s = {\n                \"id\": r[\"id\"],\n                \"name\": r[\"name\"],\n                \"category\": r[\"category\"] or \"\",\n                \"notes\": r[\"notes\"] or \"\",\n                \"container\": parent_container\n            }\n            specifics_map[r[\"id\"]] = s\n            specifics_list.append(s)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 3. Fetch all quotes (Table 1)\n    quotes_map = {}\n    tags_pool = set()\n    sentiments_pool = set()\n\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            SELECT id, idx, main_content, context, date,\n                   COALESCE(specific_ids, '[]') as specific_ids,\n                   COALESCE(preference, '[]') as preference,\n                   COALESCE(backlinks, '[]') as backlinks,\n                   COALESCE(tags, '[]') as tags,\n                   COALESCE(sentiment, '') as sentiment\n            FROM quotes\n            ORDER BY idx ASC;\n        \"\"\")\n\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            qid = r[\"id\"]\n\n            # Parse specific IDs and resolve to full Specific + Container objects\n            specific_ids = json.loads(r[\"specific_ids\"]) if r[\"specific_ids\"] else []\n            resolved_specifics = []\n            for sid in specific_ids:\n                if sid in specifics_map:\n                    resolved_specifics.append(specifics_map[sid])\n                else:\n                    resolved_specifics.append({\"id\": sid, \"name\": sid, \"container\": {\"id\": \"\", \"name\": \"Unknown\"}})\n\n            preference = json.loads(r[\"preference\"]) if r[\"preference\"] else []\n            backlinks = json.loads(r[\"backlinks\"]) if r[\"backlinks\"] else []\n\n            raw_tags = json.loads(r[\"tags\"]) if r[\"tags\"] else []\n            tags = raw_tags if isinstance(raw_tags, list) else []\n            for t in tags:\n                if isinstance(t, str) and t.strip():\n                    tags_pool.add(t.strip())\n\n            sentiment = r[\"sentiment\"] or \"\"\n            if sentiment:\n                sentiments_pool.add(sentiment.strip())\n\n            quotes_map[qid] = {\n                \"id\": qid,\n                \"index\": r[\"idx\"],\n                \"main_content\": r[\"main_content\"],\n                \"context\": r[\"context\"] or \"\",\n                \"date\": r[\"date\"],\n                \"specifics\": resolved_specifics,\n                \"preference\": preference,\n                \"backlinks\": backlinks,\n                \"tags\": tags,\n                \"sentiment\": sentiment\n            }\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    conn.close()\n\n    # Write quotes_map.json\n    quotes_map_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"quotes_map.json\")\n    with open(quotes_map_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(quotes_map, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    # Write taxonomy_index.json\n    taxonomy = {\n        \"containers\": containers_list,\n        \"specifics\": specifics_list,\n        \"tags\": sorted(list(tags_pool)),\n        \"sentiments\": sorted(list(sentiments_pool))\n    }\n    taxonomy_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"taxonomy_index.json\")\n    with open(taxonomy_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(taxonomy, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    return {\"quotes\": len(quotes_map), \"specifics\": len(specifics_list), \"containers\": len(containers_list)}\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    stats = compile_projections()\n    print(f\"[OK] Compiled projections: {stats['quotes']} quotes, {stats['specifics']} specifics, {stats['containers']} containers.\")\n"}

{"Write":"C:\\Users\\Admin\\.claude\\plans\\are-there-alternative-options-buzzing-gizmo.md: # Prescripts — Taxonomy & Tagging Distribution Plan\n\n## Context & The Core Problem\nThe Manager identified a foundational principle of clean information architecture:\n> *\"If we just gonna recite what could just be looked up via the link between tables, then it's really stupid and would create more chaos when something changes. With the 2nd and 3rd table we made, we can now exclude the 'Type' and 'Origin' from the tagging system. Now we should focus on how we would want to use tags. Maybe we put some tags in the 3rd table even. But first, we research and plan.\"*\n\n---\n\n## 1. Community Prior Art: \"Links vs Tags\" (PKM / Obsidian Consensus)\n\nResearch across the [r/ObsidianMD](https://reddit.com/r/ObsidianMD) and [Obsidian Forum](https://forum.obsidian.md) communities establishes a cardinal rule:\n\n> **\"Links/Entities are for nouns and knowledge (Who, Where, What); Tags are for verbs, purpose, and workflow (Why, How to use).\"**\n\nWhen systems duplicate entity metadata into tags (e.g. tagging `#WilliamBlake` or `#poem` when the quote is already linked to the poem *Infant Joy* by William Blake), they suffer from **The Tag Graveyard**:\n- Renaming an author or work breaks tags or creates fragmented duplicates (`#william-blake` vs `#blake`).\n- Tags become cluttered with static bibliographical facts that SQL relational joins already solve in $O(1)$ time.\n\n---\n\n## 2. The 3-Tier Layered Responsibility Matrix\n\nBy cleanly distributing metadata across our 3 tables, zero redundancy occurs:\n\n```\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                   METADATA RESPONSIBILITY MATRIX                       │\n├────────────────────┬────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────┤\n│ Tier               │ Database Table     │ What It Owns                 │\n├────────────────────┼────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤\n│ 1. Origin / Source │ Table 2:           │ • Creator / Franchise Name   │\n│                    │ containers         │ • Medium Type (book, anime)  │\n├────────────────────┼────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤\n│ 2. Specific Entity │ Table 3:           │ • Specific Work / Title      │\n│                    │ specifics          │ • Category (poem, song, diary)│\n│                    │                    │ • Optional Entity Tags       │\n├────────────────────┼────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────┤\n│ 3. Wisdom Passage  │ Table 1:           │ • Actionable Purpose / Mode  │\n│    (The Quote)     │ quotes             │ • Universal Conceptual Theme │\n│                    │                    │ • Emotional Sentiment        │\n└────────────────────┴────────────────────┴──────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Recommended Quote-Level Taxonomy (What Tags ACTUALLY Do)\n\nBecause Author, Work, Medium, and Date are already handled by the relational structure, **`quotes.tags`** and **`quotes.sentiment`** are reserved strictly for **Morning Journaling & Writing Therapy Utility**:\n\n### Dimension A: Actionable Purpose (`#purpose/...` or direct tag)\nDirectly fulfills the Manager's two defined book purposes and daily writing triggers:\n1. **`#rewrite`** *(Purpose 1: Craft Study)*\n   - Passages saved specifically to hone prose, syntax, and sentence structure by rewriting them.\n2. **`#prompt`** *(Purpose 2: Diary / Creative Spark)*\n   - Emotional sparks that trigger immediate journaling or narrative exploration.\n3. **`#anchor`** *(Personal Memory Retention)*\n   - High-value reflections from your own Diary to ensure life lessons are never forgotten.\n4. **`#memento`** *(Levity & Perspective)*\n   - Facts, curiosities, or memes that puncture morning grogginess with levity or cosmic perspective.\n5. **`#rule`** *(Meta-Habit & Principle)*\n   - Actionable personal laws (e.g. *\"Read before writing to unlock momentum\"*).\n\n### Dimension B: Universal Conceptual Themes (Cross-Cutting Ideation)\nTags that bridge completely different authors together across human history:\n- `#impermanence`, `#clarity`, `#friction`, `#craftsmanship`, `#solitude`, `#symmetry`, `#leverage`, `#focus`, `#entropy`\n\n### Dimension C: Sentiment (Emotional Frequency)\nThe psychological resonance the quote carries:\n- `stoic` (Restrained, resolute, unflinching)\n- `nostalgic` (Warm, acoustic, temporal suspension)\n- `analytical` (Cold, structural, cybernetic)\n- `defiant` (Triumphant, rebellious, high energy)\n- `playful` (Meme, absurdity, humor)\n\n---\n\n## 4. Should Table 3 (`specifics`) Have Its Own Tags?\n\n**Yes, optionally.** \nIn Table 3 (`specifics`), we already have the **`category`** column (e.g. `poem`, `music`, `book`, `diary`, `wisdom`, `character`).\nIf a Specific work spans multiple genres or sub-attributes (e.g. an anime that is `cyberpunk` and `mecha`), `specifics` can hold an optional `tags` column so those work-level genres don't have to be re-typed onto every quote extracted from that work!\n\n---\n\n## 5. Verification & Workflow in UI\n- In `prescripts_entry.html`:\n  - When picking *Infant Joy*, the quote automatically knows it's a `poem` by `William Blake`.\n  - The Tag suggestor only needs to ask you: **What is the purpose/theme?** (e.g. `rewrite`, `prompt`, `innocence`).\n  - No typing of author names or media types as tags. Zero clutter. Zero drift.\n"}

{"Write":"C:\\Users\\Admin\\.claude\\plans\\are-there-alternative-options-buzzing-gizmo.md: # Prescripts — Default Tag Pre-Fill & Authoritative Quote Tags Plan\n\n## Context & Vision\nThe Manager has designed an exceptionally clean tagging paradigm:\n> *\"How about the tags in the 2nd and 3rd table are for 'default tags' that we can just deselect if we don't want them? By doing this, only the tags in the 1st table matter at the end.\"*\n\nThis achieves two seemingly opposing software engineering goals simultaneously:\n1. **Zero-Friction Authoring**: Selecting a work or creator pre-populates sensible default tags so the Manager doesn't have to re-type or brainstorm them every time.\n2. **Absolute Runtime Simplicity**: At query time (Obsidian Dataview, ESP32 OLED, search), **only Table 1 (`quotes.tags`) is evaluated**. There is zero runtime inheritance overhead, zero join complexity, and zero risk of tag inheritance drift.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Architectural Distribution\n\n```\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│             TABLE 2: containers (Template)            │\n│  • default_tags: [\"music\", \"acoustic\"]                │\n└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘\n                            │\n                            │ (Inherited Defaults)\n                            ▼\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│             TABLE 3: specifics (Template)             │\n│  • default_tags: [\"lyricism\", \"temporal-suspension\"]  │\n└───────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────┘\n                            │\n               UI Pre-Fills into Tag Board\n               (Manager can deselect any tag with 1 click)\n                            │\n                            ▼\n┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│             TABLE 1: quotes (AUTHORITY)                │\n│  • tags: [\"music\", \"lyricism\", \"nostalgic\", \"rewrite\"] │\n│                                                        │\n│  ★ ONLY THIS FIELD MATTERS AT RUNTIME & QUERY TIME     │\n└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Database Schema Adjustments\n\n### Table 2: `containers`\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS containers (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,             -- 0002 + 12-char Unix ms\n    name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,       -- e.g. \"William Blake\", \"Vinyl Angel\", \"The Manager\"\n    type TEXT,                       -- e.g. \"author\", \"persona\", \"personal\", \"anime\"\n    default_tags TEXT,               -- JSON array: [\"poetry\", \"romanticism\"]\n    notes TEXT\n);\n```\n\n### Table 3: `specifics`\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS specifics (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,             -- 0003 + 12-char Unix ms\n    name TEXT NOT NULL,              -- e.g. \"Infant Joy\", \"Koi no Koe\", \"Diary\", \"Wisdom\"\n    container_id TEXT NOT NULL,      -- Foreign key -> containers.id\n    category TEXT,                   -- e.g. \"poem\", \"music\", \"diary\", \"wisdom\"\n    default_tags TEXT,               -- JSON array: [\"innocence\", \"joy\"]\n    notes TEXT,\n    FOREIGN KEY (container_id) REFERENCES containers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n);\n```\n\n### Table 1: `quotes` (Unchanged — Pure Authority)\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS quotes (\n    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,             -- 0001 + 12-char Unix ms\n    idx INTEGER UNIQUE NOT NULL,     -- Sequence counter (1, 2, 3...)\n    main_content TEXT NOT NULL,      -- Quote text\n    context TEXT,                    -- Genesis reflection\n    date TEXT NOT NULL,              -- Flexible date\n    specific_ids TEXT,               -- JSON array: [\"0003...\"]\n    preference TEXT,                 -- JSON array: [\"0001...\"]\n    backlinks TEXT,                  -- JSON array: [\"https://...\"]\n    tags TEXT,                       -- JSON array: Definitive active tags for this quote\n    sentiment TEXT                   -- Free-form tone\n);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. UI Workflow in `prescripts_entry.html`\n\n1. **Selection Trigger**:\n   - When the user selects a Specific Endpoint (e.g. `Infant Joy · William Blake`):\n   - The UI reads `specific.default_tags` and `container.default_tags`.\n   - It merges them into `activeQuote.tags`.\n2. **Visual Choice Board**:\n   - The tags appear immediately in Section 5 as active (amber-highlighted) pills.\n   - If the user doesn't want one (e.g. they don't want `#romanticism` for this specific stanza), they click the pill once to **deselect it**.\n3. **Custom Additions**:\n   - The user can type any custom tag into the suggestor (e.g. `#rewrite`, `#prompt`) and press Enter to add it.\n4. **Authoritative Commitment**:\n   - When \"Save Quote\" is clicked, only the tags currently active in the Tag Board are committed into `quotes.tags`.\n   - Projections (`quotes_map.json`) compile `quotes.tags` directly.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Implementation Steps\n\n1. **Update `D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\db.py`**:\n   - Add `default_tags` column to `containers` and `specifics`.\n   - Update `upsert_container` and `upsert_specific` to store `default_tags`.\n   - Update `get_all_specifics()` to include parent container's `default_tags`.\n2. **Update `D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\seed_quote.py`**:\n   - Seed sensible default tags on core containers and specifics:\n     - `William Blake`: `[\"poetry\", \"romanticism\"]`\n     - `Infant Joy`: `[\"innocence\", \"joy\"]`\n     - `Vinyl Angel`: `[\"music\", \"acoustic\"]`\n     - `Koi no Koe`: `[\"lyricism\", \"temporal-suspension\"]`\n     - `The Manager`: `[\"personal\"]`\n     - `Diary`: `[\"diary\", \"anchor\"]`\n     - `Wisdom`: `[\"wisdom\", \"rule\"]`\n3. **Update `D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\compile_projections.py`**:\n   - Include `default_tags` in `taxonomy_index.json` under `containers` and `specifics`.\n4. **Update `D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\prescripts_entry.html`**:\n   - In `toggleSpecific(id)`: When an endpoint is selected, auto-merge its `default_tags` and container's `default_tags` into `activeQuote.tags`.\n   - Render them in the active Tag Board ready for 1-click deselect.\n5. **Verification**:\n   - Test picking `Infant Joy` in browser $\\rightarrow$ observe default tags populate.\n   - Deselect a tag, save, and verify SQLite `quotes.tags` contains the exact desired subset.\n"}

{"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\db.py: \"\"\"\nEntropy Rose / Prescripts: Pure 3-Tier Entity SQLite Engine.\nEstablishes the universal Container -> Specific -> Quote hierarchy:\n  1. 'containers' (Prefix 0002... : Origin / Franchise / Creator / Persona)\n     - default_tags: authoring template tags\n  2. 'specifics'  (Prefix 0003... : Specific Endpoint / Work / Character / Diary / Wisdom)\n     - default_tags: work-level template tags\n  3. 'quotes'     (Prefix 0001... : Wisdom Quote Artifact, references specific_ids)\n     - tags: sole authoritative runtime tags\nZero redundant shadow tables, zero duplicate timestamps, zero dead columns.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sqlite3\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional\n\nBASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nDEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\", \"rose_quotes.db\")\nDEFAULT_JSONL_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\", \"quotes.jsonl\")\n\n\ndef get_connection(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> sqlite3.Connection:\n    \"\"\"Creates a connection configured with WAL and concurrency pragmas.\"\"\"\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(db_path)), exist_ok=True)\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path, timeout=5.0)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;\")\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;\")\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000;\")\n    conn.execute(\"PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;\")\n    return conn\n\n\ndef init_db(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Initializes the pure 3-table relational schema.\"\"\"\n    conn = get_connection(db_path)\n    with conn:\n        conn.execute(\"BEGIN IMMEDIATE;\")\n\n        # Drop legacy speakers table if present\n        conn.execute(\"DROP TABLE IF EXISTS speakers;\")\n\n        # Table 2: containers (id: 0002..., name, type, default_tags, notes)\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS containers (\n                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n                name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n                type TEXT,\n                default_tags TEXT,\n                notes TEXT\n            );\n        \"\"\")\n        conn.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_containers_name ON containers(name);\")\n\n        c_cols = [r[\"name\"] for r in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(containers);\").fetchall()]\n        if c_cols and \"default_tags\" not in c_cols:\n            conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE containers ADD COLUMN default_tags TEXT;\")\n\n        # Table 3: specifics (id: 0003..., name, container_id, category, default_tags, notes)\n        conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS specifics (\n                id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n                name TEXT NOT NULL,\n                container_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n                category TEXT,\n                default_tags TEXT,\n                notes TEXT,\n                FOREIGN KEY (container_id) REFERENCES containers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE\n            );\n        \"\"\")\n        conn.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_specifics_container ON specifics(container_id);\")\n        conn.execute(\"CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_specifics_name ON specifics(name);\")\n\n        s_cols = [r[\"name\"] for r in conn.execute(\"PRAGMA table_info(specifics);\").fetchall()]\n        if s_cols and \"default_tags\" not in s_cols:\n            conn.execute(\"ALTER TABLE specifics ADD COLUMN default_tags TEXT;\")\n\n        #
