{"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\compile_projections.py: \"\"\"\nEntropy Rose / Prescripts: Deterministic CQRS Projection Compiler.\nCompiles the pure 3-table relational schema:\n  quotes -> specifics -> containers\ninto O(1) denormalized JSON projection maps for Obsidian and edge clients.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sqlite3\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, List\n\nBASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nDEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\", \"rose_quotes.db\")\nDEFAULT_OUT_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\")\n\n\ndef compile_projections(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH, out_dir: str = DEFAULT_OUT_DIR) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Reads authoritative SQLite tables and generates:\n      1. quotes_map.json: { [hex_id]: QuoteObject } (Denormalized with joined Specifics & Containers for O(1) recite)\n      2. taxonomy_index.json: { containers: [...], specifics: [...], tags: [...], sentiments: [...] }\n    \"\"\"\n    if not os.path.exists(db_path):\n        return {\"quotes\": 0, \"specifics\": 0, \"containers\": 0}\n\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n\n    tags_pool = set()\n    sentiments_pool = set()\n\n    # 1. Fetch all containers (Table 2) with default_tags\n    containers_map = {}\n    containers_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, type, COALESCE(default_tags, '[]') as default_tags, notes FROM containers ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            c_tags = json.loads(r[\"default_tags\"]) if r[\"default_tags\"] else []\n            for t in c_tags:\n                if t and str(t).strip():\n                    tags_pool.add(str(t).strip())\n            c = {\n                \"id\": r[\"id\"],\n                \"name\": r[\"name\"],\n                \"type\": r[\"type\"] or \"\",\n                \"default_tags\": c_tags,\n                \"notes\": r[\"notes\"] or \"\"\n            }\n            containers_map[r[\"id\"]] = c\n            containers_list.append(c)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 2. Fetch all specifics (Table 3) pre-joined with containers and default_tags\n    specifics_map = {}\n    specifics_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, container_id, category, COALESCE(default_tags, '[]') as default_tags, notes FROM specifics ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            cid = r[\"container_id\"]\n            parent_container = containers_map.get(cid, {\"id\": cid, \"name\": cid, \"type\": \"\", \"default_tags\": []})\n            s_tags = json.loads(r[\"default_tags\"]) if r[\"default_tags\"] else []\n            for t in s_tags:\n                if t and str(t).strip():\n                    tags_pool.add(str(t).strip())\n            s = {\n                \"id\": r[\"id\"],\n                \"name\": r[\"name\"],\n                \"category\": r[\"category\"] or \"\",\n                \"default_tags\": s_tags,\n                \"container_default_tags\": parent_container.get(\"default_tags\", []),\n                \"notes\": r[\"notes\"] or \"\",\n                \"container\": parent_container\n            }\n            specifics_map[r[\"id\"]] = s\n            specifics_list.append(s)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 3. Fetch all quotes (Table 1) - ONLY Table 1 holds authoritative runtime tags\n    quotes_map = {}\n\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            SELECT id, idx, main_content, context, date,\n                   COALESCE(specific_ids, '[]') as specific_ids,\n                   COALESCE(preference, '[]') as preference,\n                   COALESCE(backlinks, '[]') as backlinks,\n                   COALESCE(tags, '[]') as tags,\n                   COALESCE(sentiment, '') as sentiment\n            FROM quotes\n            ORDER BY idx ASC;\n        \"\"\")\n\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            qid = r[\"id\"]\n\n            # Parse specific IDs and resolve to full Specific + Container objects\n            specific_ids = json.loads(r[\"specific_ids\"]) if r[\"specific_ids\"] else []\n            resolved_specifics = []\n            for sid in specific_ids:\n                if sid in specifics_map:\n                    resolved_specifics.append(specifics_map[sid])\n                else:\n                    resolved_specifics.append({\"id\": sid, \"name\": sid, \"container\": {\"id\": \"\", \"name\": \"Unknown\"}})\n\n            preference = json.loads(r[\"preference\"]) if r[\"preference\"] else []\n            backlinks = json.loads(r[\"backlinks\"]) if r[\"backlinks\"] else []\n\n            # Quotes table holds the final authoritative tags\n            raw_tags = json.loads(r[\"tags\"]) if r[\"tags\"] else []\n            tags = raw_tags if isinstance(raw_tags, list) else []\n            for t in tags:\n                if isinstance(t, str) and t.strip():\n                    tags_pool.add(t.strip())\n\n            sentiment = r[\"sentiment\"] or \"\"\n            if sentiment:\n                sentiments_pool.add(sentiment.strip())\n\n            quotes_map[qid] = {\n                \"id\": qid,\n                \"index\": r[\"idx\"],\n                \"main_content\": r[\"main_content\"],\n                \"context\": r[\"context\"] or \"\",\n                \"date\": r[\"date\"],\n                \"specifics\": resolved_specifics,\n                \"preference\": preference,\n                \"backlinks\": backlinks,\n                \"tags\": tags,\n                \"sentiment\": sentiment\n            }\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    conn.close()\n\n    # Write quotes_map.json\n    quotes_map_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"quotes_map.json\")\n    with open(quotes_map_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(quotes_map, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    # Write taxonomy_index.json\n    taxonomy = {\n        \"containers\": containers_list,\n        \"specifics\": specifics_list,\n        \"tags\": sorted(list(tags_pool)),\n        \"sentiments\": sorted(list(sentiments_pool))\n    }\n    taxonomy_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"taxonomy_index.json\")\n    with open(taxonomy_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(taxonomy, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    return {\"quotes\": len(quotes_map), \"specifics\": len(specifics_list), \"containers\": len(containers_list)}\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    stats = compile_projections()\n    print(f\"[OK] Compiled projections: {stats['quotes']} quotes, {stats['specifics']} specifics, {stats['containers']} containers.\")\n"}

{"Write":"D:\\My Drive\\Blue AI\\Entropy\\Rose\\compile_projections.py: \"\"\"\nEntropy Rose / Prescripts: Deterministic CQRS Projection Compiler.\nCompiles the pure 4-table relational schema:\n  quotes -> specifics -> containers + themes (child_of tree)\ninto O(1) denormalized JSON projection maps for Obsidian and edge clients.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport sqlite3\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom typing import Dict, Any, List\n\nBASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nDEFAULT_DB_PATH = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\", \"rose_quotes.db\")\nDEFAULT_OUT_DIR = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, \"data\")\n\n\ndef compile_projections(db_path: str = DEFAULT_DB_PATH, out_dir: str = DEFAULT_OUT_DIR) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Reads authoritative SQLite tables and generates:\n      1. quotes_map.json: { [hex_id]: QuoteObject } (Denormalized with Specifics, Containers & Themes)\n      2. taxonomy_index.json: { containers: [...], specifics: [...], themes: [...], tags: [...] }\n    \"\"\"\n    if not os.path.exists(db_path):\n        return {\"quotes\": 0, \"specifics\": 0, \"containers\": 0, \"themes\": 0}\n\n    os.makedirs(out_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\n    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\n\n    tags_pool = set()\n\n    # 1. Fetch all containers (Table 2)\n    containers_map = {}\n    containers_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, type, COALESCE(default_tags, '[]') as default_tags, notes FROM containers ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            c_tags = json.loads(r[\"default_tags\"]) if r[\"default_tags\"] else []\n            for t in c_tags:\n                if t and str(t).strip():\n                    tags_pool.add(str(t).strip())\n            c = {\n                \"id\": r[\"id\"],\n                \"name\": r[\"name\"],\n                \"type\": r[\"type\"] or \"\",\n                \"default_tags\": c_tags,\n                \"notes\": r[\"notes\"] or \"\"\n            }\n            containers_map[r[\"id\"]] = c\n            containers_list.append(c)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 2. Fetch all specifics (Table 3) pre-joined with containers\n    specifics_map = {}\n    specifics_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, container_id, category, COALESCE(default_tags, '[]') as default_tags, notes FROM specifics ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            cid = r[\"container_id\"]\n            parent_container = containers_map.get(cid, {\"id\": cid, \"name\": cid, \"type\": \"\", \"default_tags\": []})\n            s_tags = json.loads(r[\"default_tags\"]) if r[\"default_tags\"] else []\n            for t in s_tags:\n                if t and str(t).strip():\n                    tags_pool.add(str(t).strip())\n            s = {\n                \"id\": r[\"id\"],\n                \"name\": r[\"name\"],\n                \"category\": r[\"category\"] or \"\",\n                \"default_tags\": s_tags,\n                \"container_default_tags\": parent_container.get(\"default_tags\", []),\n                \"notes\": r[\"notes\"] or \"\",\n                \"container\": parent_container\n            }\n            specifics_map[r[\"id\"]] = s\n            specifics_list.append(s)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 3. Fetch all themes (Table 4) with child_of tree relation\n    themes_map = {}\n    themes_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            SELECT t.id, t.name, t.child_of, t.notes, p.name as parent_name\n            FROM themes t\n            LEFT JOIN themes p ON t.child_of = p.id\n            ORDER BY t.name ASC;\n        \"\"\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            th = {\n                \"id\": r[\"id\"],\n                \"name\": r[\"name\"],\n                \"child_of\": r[\"child_of\"],\n                \"parent_name\": r[\"parent_name\"] or \"\",\n                \"notes\": r[\"notes\"] or \"\"\n            }\n            themes_map[r[\"id\"]] = th\n            themes_list.append(th)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 4. Fetch all quotes (Table 1)\n    quotes_map = {}\n\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"\"\"\n            SELECT id, idx, main_content, context, date,\n                   specific_id,\n                   COALESCE(theme_ids, '[]') as theme_ids,\n                   COALESCE(usage, '[]') as usage,\n                   COALESCE(preference, '[]') as preference,\n                   COALESCE(backlinks, '[]') as backlinks,\n                   COALESCE(tags, '[]') as tags,\n                   COALESCE(sentiment, '') as sentiment\n            FROM quotes\n            ORDER BY idx ASC;\n        \"\"\")\n\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            qid = r[\"id\"]\n            sid = r[\"specific_id\"]\n            resolved_specific = specifics_map.get(sid, None)\n\n            # Resolve theme objects\n            theme_ids = json.loads(r[\"theme_ids\"]) if r[\"theme_ids\"] else []\n            resolved_themes = [themes_map[tid] for tid in theme_ids if tid in themes_map]\n\n            usage = json.loads(r[\"usage\"]) if r[\"usage\"] else []\n            preference = json.loads(r[\"preference\"]) if r[\"preference\"] else []\n            backlinks = json.loads(r[\"backlinks\"]) if r[\"backlinks\"] else []\n\n            raw_tags = json.loads(r[\"tags\"]) if r[\"tags\"] else []\n            tags = raw_tags if isinstance(raw_tags, list) else []\n            for t in tags:\n                if isinstance(t, str) and t.strip():\n                    tags_pool.add(t.strip())\n\n            quotes_map[qid] = {\n                \"id\": qid,\n                \"index\": r[\"idx\"],\n                \"main_content\": r[\"main_content\"],\n                \"context\": r[\"context\"] or \"\",\n                \"date\": r[\"date\"],\n                \"specific\": resolved_specific,\n                \"themes\": resolved_themes,\n                \"usage\": usage,\n                \"preference\": preference,\n                \"backlinks\": backlinks,\n                \"tags\": tags,\n                \"sentiment\": r[\"sentiment\"] or \"\"\n            }\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    conn.close()\n\n    # Write quotes_map.json\n    quotes_map_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"quotes_map.json\")\n    with open(quotes_map_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(quotes_map, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    # Write taxonomy_index.json\n    taxonomy = {\n        \"containers\": containers_list,\n        \"specifics\": specifics_list,\n        \"themes\": themes_list,\n        \"tags\": sorted(list(tags_pool))\n    }\n    taxonomy_path = os.path.join(out_dir, \"taxonomy_index.json\")\n    with open(taxonomy_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        json.dump(taxonomy, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)\n\n    return {\n        \"quotes\": len(quotes_map),\n        \"specifics\": len(specifics_list),\n        \"containers\": len(containers_list),\n        \"themes\": len(themes_list)\n    }\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    stats = compile_projections()\n    print(f\"[OK] Compiled: {stats['quotes']} quotes, {stats['specifics']} specifics, {stats['containers']} containers, {stats['themes']} themes.\")\n"}

Toast Message -->\n  <div class=\"toast-msg\" id=\"toast\">State Updated</div>\n\n\n  <!-- ======================================================================\n       APPLICATION JAVASCRIPT LOGIC\n       ====================================================================== -->\n  <script>\n    const API_BASE = window.location.origin.startsWith('http') ? window.location.origin : 'http://127.0.0.1:20136';\n\n    // State\n    let activeQuote = {\n      id: \"\",\n      index: 1,\n      main_content: \"\",\n      context: \"\",\n      date: \"\",\n      specific_id: null,\n      theme_ids: [],\n      usage: [],\n      preference: [],\n      backlinks: [],\n      tags: [],\n      sentiment: \"\"\n    };\n\n    let activeDefaultTags = [];\n\n    // Master Cache\n    let availableContainers = [];\n    let availableSpecifics = [];\n    let availableThemes = [];\n    let availableQuotes = {};\n    let allQuotesList = [];\n\n    // Usage Options with Conflict Rules\n    const USAGE_OPTIONS = [\n      { id: \"rewrite\", label: \"✍ Rewrite Craft\", desc: \"Sentence rhythm & prose training\", conflicts: [\"rule\"] },\n      { id: \"prompt\", label: \"💡 Diary Prompt\", desc: \"Creative catalyst for morning writing\", conflicts: [] },\n      { id: \"anchor\", label: \"⚓ Memory Anchor\", desc: \"Core realization from personal history\", conflicts: [\"memento\"] },\n      { id: \"rule\", label: \"📜 Daily Rule\", desc: \"Actionable principle & meta-habit\", conflicts: [\"rewrite\"] },\n      { id: \"memento\", label: \"🪐 Perspective / Levity\", desc: \"Cosmic scale / meme / curiosity\", conflicts: [\"anchor\"] }\n    ];\n\n    // Hex Generator\n    function generateHexId(prefix = \"0001\") {\n      const now = Date.now();\n      const hexTs = now.toString(16).toUpperCase().padStart(12, '0').slice(-12);\n      const cleanPrefix = String(prefix).toUpperCase().padStart(4, '0').slice(0, 4);\n      return `${cleanPrefix}${hexTs}`;\n    }\n\n    // View Switcher\n    function switchView(viewId, btn) {\n      document.querySelectorAll('.view-container').forEach(v => v.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.querySelectorAll('.nav-tab-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));\n      document.getElementById(viewId).classList.add('active');\n      if (btn) btn.classList.add('active');\n\n      if (viewId === 'containers-view') renderContainersTable();\n      if (viewId === 'specifics-view') renderSpecificsTable();\n      if (viewId === 'themes-view') renderThemesTree();\n      if (viewId === 'archive-view') renderArchiveTable();\n    }\n\n    function fireToast(msg) {\n      const t = document.getElementById('toast');\n      t.textContent = msg;\n      t.classList.add('show');\n      setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2400);\n    }\n\n    function closeAllDropdowns() {\n      document.querySelectorAll('.notion-dropdown-menu').forEach(dd => dd.classList.remove('open'));\n      document.querySelectorAll('.notion-combobox-trigger').forEach(tr => tr.classList.remove('active'));\n    }\n\n    function updateField(field, value) {\n      activeQuote[field] = value;\n      if (!activeQuote.id && activeQuote.main_content.trim()) {\n        activeQuote.id = generateHexId(\"0001\");\n      }\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function updateBacklink(val) {\n      activeQuote.backlinks = val.trim() ? [val.trim()] : [];\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function renderLiveJSON() {\n      const pre = document.getElementById('live-json-pre');\n      pre.textContent = JSON.stringify(activeQuote, null, 2);\n      document.getElementById('live-index-badge').textContent = `IDX: ${activeQuote.index}`;\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 2. DATE SUGGESTOR\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function handleDateInput(val) {\n      activeQuote.date = val.trim();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('date-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('date-options-list');\n      const q = val.trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();\n      let suggestions = [];\n\n      if (!q) {\n        suggestions = [`${currentYear}`, `${currentYear}-10`, `${currentYear}-10-02`, `2020~${currentYear}`, `2020`];\n      } else {\n        if (/^\\d{1,4}$/.test(q)) {\n          suggestions.push(q, `${q}-01`, `${q}-05`, `${q}~${currentYear}`);\n        } else if (/^\\d{4}-/.test(q)) {\n          suggestions.push(q, `${q}-01`, `${q}-15`);\n        } else {\n          suggestions.push(q);\n        }\n      }\n\n      list.innerHTML = suggestions.slice(0, 6).map(s => `\n        <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row\" onclick=\"selectDate('${s}')\">\n          <span style=\"color:var(--accent); font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-weight:600;\">📅 ${s}</span>\n        </div>\n      `).join('');\n    }\n\n    function selectDate(val) {\n      document.getElementById('input-date').value = val;\n      activeQuote.date = val;\n      document.getElementById('date-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function clearDateInput() {\n      document.getElementById('input-date').value = \"\";\n      activeQuote.date = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('date-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function handleDateKey(e) {\n      if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === 'Escape') {\n        document.getElementById('date-dropdown').classList.remove('open');\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 3. SPECIFIC SINGLE SELECTOR\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function toggleSpecificDropdown() {\n      const menu = document.getElementById('specific-combo-menu');\n      const trigger = document.getElementById('specific-combo-trigger');\n      if (!menu || !trigger) return;\n      const isOpen = menu.classList.contains('open');\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      if (!isOpen) {\n        menu.classList.add('open');\n        trigger.classList.add('active');\n        const searchInput = document.getElementById('specific-search-input');\n        if (searchInput) {\n          searchInput.value = \"\";\n          filterSpecificDropdown(\"\");\n          searchInput.focus();\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    function selectSpecific(id) {\n      if (activeDefaultTags.length > 0) {\n        activeQuote.tags = activeQuote.tags.filter(t => !activeDefaultTags.includes(t));\n        activeDefaultTags = [];\n      }\n\n      activeQuote.specific_id = id;\n\n      const s = availableSpecifics.find(item => item.id === id);\n      if (s) {\n        const defaults = [\n          ...(s.default_tags || []),\n          ...(s.container_default_tags || (s.container && s.container.default_tags ? s.container.default_tags : []))\n        ].map(t => String(t).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean);\n\n        activeDefaultTags = [...defaults];\n        defaults.forEach(t => {\n          if (!activeQuote.tags.includes(t)) activeQuote.tags.push(t);\n        });\n\n        renderSpecificBadge(s);\n        fireToast(`Selected: ${s.name} · ${s.container ? s.container.name : ''}`);\n      }\n\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function clearSpecificSelection() {\n      if (activeDefaultTags.length > 0) {\n        activeQuote.tags = activeQuote.tags.filter(t => !activeDefaultTags.includes(t));\n        activeDefaultTags = [];\n      }\n      activeQuote.specific_id = null;\n      renderSpecificBadge(null);\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function renderSpecificBadge(s) {\n      const badge = document.getElementById('specific-selected-val');\n      const clearBtn = document.getElementById('specific-clear-btn');\n      if (s) {\n        const cname = s.container ? s.container.name : '';\n        badge.innerHTML = `<span class=\"selected-val-badge\">📍 ${s.name} · <span style=\"opacity:0.75; font-weight:normal;\">${cname}</span></span>`;\n        if (clearBtn) clearBtn.style.display = 'inline-flex';\n      } else {\n        badge.innerHTML = `<span style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.9rem;\">Select specific endpoint...</span>`;\n        if (clearBtn) clearBtn.style.display = 'none';\n      }\n    }\n\n    function filterSpecificDropdown(q) {\n      const list = document.getElementById('specific-options-list');\n      const createRow = document.getElementById('specific-create-row');\n      const query = (q || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      const matches = availableSpecifics.filter(s => {\n        const sName = (s.name || \"\").toLowerCase();\n        const cName = (s.container && s.container.name) ? s.container.name.toLowerCase() : '';\n        return sName.includes(query) || cName.includes(query);\n      });\n\n      if (matches.length === 0) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.85rem; padding:0.5rem;\">No matching endpoints.</div>';\n      } else {\n        list.innerHTML = matches.map(s => {\n          const isSelected = activeQuote.specific_id === s.id;\n          const cname = (s.container && s.container.name) ? ` · ${s.container.name}` : '';\n          return `\n            <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}\" onclick=\"selectSpecific('${s.id}')\">\n              <div class=\"opt-label-group\">\n                <div class=\"opt-title-row\">\n                  ${isSelected ? '<span class=\"opt-check\">✓</span>' : ''}\n                  <span class=\"opt-title\">${s.name}</span>\n                  <span class=\"opt-context\">${cname}</span>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          `;\n        }).join('');\n      }\n\n      if (query && !availableSpecifics.some(s => (s.name || \"\").toLowerCase() === query)) {\n        createRow.style.display = 'flex';\n        document.getElementById('specific-create-query').textContent = q.trim();\n      } else {\n        createRow.style.display = 'none';\n      }\n    }\n\n    function handleSpecificKey(e) {\n      if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n        const q = (document.getElementById('specific-search-input').value || \"\").trim();\n        const match = availableSpecifics.find(s => (s.name || \"\").toLowerCase() === q.toLowerCase());\n        if (match) selectSpecific(match.id);\n        else openCreateSpecificModal(q);\n      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n        closeAllDropdowns();\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 4. THEMES TREE MULTI-SELECT\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderThemeChips() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('theme-chips');\n      container.innerHTML = activeQuote.theme_ids.map(tid => {\n        const th = availableThemes.find(t => t.id === tid);\n        const name = th ? th.name : tid;\n        const parent = (th && th.parent_name) ? ` (${th.parent_name})` : '';\n        return `\n          <span class=\"chip-badge\">\n            <span>🌲 ${name}${parent}</span>\n            <span class=\"chip-remove\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); removeTheme('${tid}')\">✕</span>\n          </span>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function filterThemeDropdown(q) {\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('theme-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('theme-options-list');\n      const createRow = document.getElementById('theme-create-row');\n      const query = (q || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const matches = availableThemes.filter(t => t.name.toLowerCase().includes(query) || (t.parent_name && t.parent_name.toLowerCase().includes(query)));\n\n      if (matches.length === 0) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.85rem; padding:0.5rem;\">No matching themes in tree.</div>';\n      } else {\n        list.innerHTML = matches.map(t => {\n          const isSelected = activeQuote.theme_ids.includes(t.id);\n          const parent = t.parent_name ? ` · ${t.parent_name}` : ' · [Root]';\n          return `\n            <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}\" onclick=\"toggleThemeSelect('${t.id}')\">\n              <div class=\"opt-label-group\">\n                <div class=\"opt-title-row\">\n                  <span class=\"opt-check\">${isSelected ? '✓' : '+'}</span>\n                  <span class=\"opt-title\">${t.name}</span>\n                  <span class=\"opt-context\">${parent}</span>\n                </div>\n              </div>\n            </div>\n          `;\n        }).join('');\n      }\n\n      if (query && !availableThemes.some(t => t.name.toLowerCase() === query)) {\n        createRow.style.display = 'flex';\n        document.getElementById('theme-create-query').textContent = q.trim();\n      } else {\n        createRow.style.display = 'none';\n      }\n    }\n\n    function toggleThemeSelect(id) {\n      if (activeQuote.theme_ids.includes(id)) {\n        activeQuote.theme_ids = activeQuote.theme_ids.filter(t => t !== id);\n      } else {\n        activeQuote.theme_ids.push(id);\n      }\n      renderThemeChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      filterThemeDropdown(document.getElementById('theme-input').value);\n    }\n\n    function removeTheme(id) {\n      activeQuote.theme_ids = activeQuote.theme_ids.filter(t => t !== id);\n      renderThemeChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function clearThemeInput() {\n      document.getElementById('theme-input').value = \"\";\n      filterThemeDropdown(\"\");\n    }\n\n    function handleThemeKey(e) {\n      if (e.key === 'Enter') {\n        const q = (document.getElementById('theme-input').value || \"\").trim();\n        const match = availableThemes.find(t => t.name.toLowerCase() === q.toLowerCase());\n        if (match) toggleThemeSelect(match.id);\n        else openCreateThemeModal(null, q);\n      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {\n        closeAllDropdowns();\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 5. USAGE MULTI-SELECT WITH CONFLICT RULES\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderUsagePills() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('usage-pills-container');\n      container.innerHTML = USAGE_OPTIONS.map(opt => {\n        const isActive = activeQuote.usage.includes(opt.id);\n        return `\n          <div class=\"btn-action ${isActive ? 'btn-primary' : ''}\" style=\"cursor:pointer; font-size:0.82rem;\" title=\"${opt.desc}\" onclick=\"toggleUsage('${opt.id}')\">\n            <span>${opt.label}</span>\n            ${isActive ? '<span>✓</span>' : ''}\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n      document.getElementById('active-usage-count').textContent = `${activeQuote.usage.length} selected`;\n    }\n\n    function toggleUsage(id) {\n      const opt = USAGE_OPTIONS.find(o => o.id === id);\n      if (!opt) return;\n\n      if (activeQuote.usage.includes(id)) {\n        activeQuote.usage = activeQuote.usage.filter(u => u !== id);\n      } else {\n        // Enforce conflict matrix: auto-remove conflicting choices!\n        if (opt.conflicts && opt.conflicts.length > 0) {\n          activeQuote.usage = activeQuote.usage.filter(u => !opt.conflicts.includes(u));\n          fireToast(`Resolved conflict for ${opt.label}`);\n        }\n        activeQuote.usage.push(id);\n      }\n      renderUsagePills();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // 6. RESPONSE QUOTES\n    // ==============================================================================\n    function renderPrefChips() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('pref-chips');\n      container.innerHTML = activeQuote.preference.map(qid => {\n        const q = availableQuotes[qid];\n        const snippet = q ? q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 36) + '...' : qid.substring(0, 8);\n        return `\n          <span class=\"chip-badge\" title=\"${q ? q.main_content : qid}\">\n            <span>💬 ${snippet}</span>\n            <span class=\"chip-remove\" onclick=\"event.stopPropagation(); removePref('${qid}')\">✕</span>\n          </span>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function filterPrefDropdown(q) {\n      const dropdown = document.getElementById('pref-dropdown');\n      const list = document.getElementById('pref-options-list');\n      const query = (q || \"\").trim().toLowerCase();\n\n      closeAllDropdowns();\n      dropdown.classList.add('open');\n\n      const matches = Object.values(availableQuotes).filter(quote => {\n        return quote.main_content.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||\n               (quote.context && quote.context.toLowerCase().includes(query));\n      });\n\n      if (matches.length === 0) {\n        list.innerHTML = '<div style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.85rem; padding:0.5rem;\">No matching quotes found.</div>';\n        return;\n      }\n\n      list.innerHTML = matches.map(quote => {\n        const isSelected = activeQuote.preference.includes(quote.id);\n        const firstLine = quote.main_content.split('\\n')[0];\n        return `\n          <div class=\"dropdown-opt-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}\" onclick=\"togglePref('${quote.id}')\">\n            <div class=\"opt-label-group\">\n              <div class=\"opt-title-row\">\n                <span class=\"opt-check\">${isSelected ? '✓' : '+'}</span>\n                <span class=\"opt-title\">“${firstLine}”</span>\n              </div>\n              <span class=\"opt-context\">${quote.date} · ${quote.context || ''}</span>\n            </div>\n            <span style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.7rem; color:var(--text-dim);\">#${quote.id.substring(0,8)}</span>\n          </div>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function togglePref(id) {\n      if (activeQuote.preference.includes(id)) activeQuote.preference = activeQuote.preference.filter(q => q !== id);\n      else activeQuote.preference.push(id);\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n      filterPrefDropdown(document.getElementById('pref-input').value);\n    }\n\n    function removePref(id) {\n      activeQuote.preference = activeQuote.preference.filter(q => q !== id);\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    function clearPrefInput() {\n      document.getElementById('pref-input').value = \"\";\n      filterPrefDropdown(\"\");\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // SAVE QUOTE (DIRECT SQLITE)\n    // ==============================================================================\n    async function saveQuote() {\n      if (!activeQuote.main_content.trim()) {\n        alert(\"Please provide the main quote content before saving.\");\n        return;\n      }\n\n      if (!activeQuote.id) activeQuote.id = generateHexId(\"0001\");\n      if (!activeQuote.date) activeQuote.date = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];\n\n      try {\n        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quote`, {\n          method: 'POST',\n          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n          body: JSON.stringify(activeQuote)\n        });\n\n        if (res.ok) {\n          const data = await res.json();\n          fireToast(`✓ Saved Quote #${data.index} to SQLite!`);\n          activeQuote.index = data.index + 1;\n          activeQuote.id = \"\";\n          activeQuote.main_content = \"\";\n          activeQuote.context = \"\";\n          activeQuote.preference = [];\n          document.getElementById('input-main-content').value = \"\";\n          document.getElementById('input-context').value = \"\";\n          renderPrefChips();\n          renderLiveJSON();\n          fetchBackendData();\n          return;\n        } else {\n          const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));\n          fireToast(\"Server Error: \" + (errData.error || res.statusText));\n        }\n      } catch (err) {\n        fireToast(\"❌ Error: Local Server unreachable.\");\n      }\n    }\n\n    function clearForm() {\n      activeDefaultTags = [];\n      activeQuote = {\n        id: \"\",\n        index: activeQuote.index,\n        main_content: \"\",\n        context: \"\",\n        date: \"\",\n        specific_id: null,\n        theme_ids: [],\n        usage: [],\n        preference: [],\n        backlinks: [],\n        tags: [],\n        sentiment: \"\"\n      };\n      document.getElementById('input-main-content').value = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('input-context').value = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('input-date').value = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('input-backlink').value = \"\";\n      renderSpecificBadge(null);\n      renderThemeChips();\n      renderUsagePills();\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    // ==============================================================================\n    // CRUD STUDIO LOGIC (VIEWS 2, 3, 4, 5)\n    // ==============================================================================\n\n    // --- CONTAINERS (VIEW 2) ---\n    function renderContainersTable() {\n      const tbody = document.getElementById('containers-table-body');\n      tbody.innerHTML = availableContainers.map(c => `\n        <tr>\n          <td style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-dim);\">${c.id}</td>\n          <td style=\"font-weight:700; color:var(--text-vivid);\">${c.name}</td>\n          <td><span class=\"card-tag\">${c.type || 'origin'}</span></td>\n          <td>${(c.default_tags || []).map(t => `<span class=\"chip-badge\" style=\"font-size:0.75rem;\">#${t}</span>`).join(' ')}</td>\n          <td style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.82rem;\">${c.notes || ''}</td>\n          <td style=\"text-align:right;\">\n            <button class=\"btn-action\" style=\"padding:0.25rem 0.5rem; font-size:0.75rem;\" onclick=\"openEditContainerModal('${c.id}')\">✏ Edit</button>\n            <button class=\"btn-action btn-danger\" style=\"padding:0.25rem 0.5rem; font-size:0.75rem;\" onclick=\"deleteContainer('${c.id}')\">🗑</button>\n          </td>\n        </tr>\n      `).join('');\n    }\n\n    function openCreateContainerModal() {\n      document.getElementById('container-modal-title').textContent = \"+ Create New Container\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-id').value = generateHexId(\"0002\");\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-name').value = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-type').value = \"author\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-tags').value = \"\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-notes').value = \"\";\n      openModal('container-modal');\n    }\n\n    function openEditContainerModal(id) {\n      const c = availableContainers.find(item => item.id === id);\n      if (!c) return;\n      document.getElementById('container-modal-title').textContent = \"✏ Edit Container\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-id').value = c.id;\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-name').value = c.name;\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-type').value = c.type || \"\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-tags').value = (c.default_tags || []).join(', ');\n      document.getElementById('modal-container-notes').value = c.notes || \"\";\n      openModal('container-modal');\n    }\n\n    async function saveContainerModal() {\n      const id = document.getElementById('modal-container-id').value;\n      const name = document.getElementById('modal-container-name').value.trim();\n      const type = document.getElementById('modal-container-type').value.trim();\n      const tags = document.getElementById('modal-container-tags').value.split(',').map(t => t.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean);\n      const notes = document.getElementById('modal-container-notes').value.trim();\n\n      if (!name) return alert(\"Container name is required.\");\n\n      const payload = { id, name, type, default_tags: tags, notes };\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/container`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify(payload)\n      });\n      if (res.ok) {\n        closeModal('container-modal');\n        fireToast(`Saved Container: ${name}`);\n        await fetchBackendData();\n        renderContainersTable();\n      }\n    }\n\n    async function deleteContainer(id) {\n      if (!confirm(\"Are you sure you want to delete this container? Specifics under it will be deleted.\")) return;\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/container/delete`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ id })\n      });\n      if (res.ok) {\n        fireToast(\"Container deleted\");\n        await fetchBackendData();\n        renderContainersTable();\n      }\n    }\n\n    // --- SPECIFICS (VIEW 3) ---\n    function renderSpecificsTable() {\n      const tbody = document.getElementById('specifics-table-body');\n      tbody.innerHTML = availableSpecifics.map(s => {\n        const cname = s.container ? s.container.name : s.container_name || '';\n        return `\n          <tr>\n            <td style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.75rem; color:var(--text-dim);\">${s.id}</td>\n            <td style=\"font-weight:700; color:var(--text-vivid);\">📍 ${s.name}</td>\n            <td><span class=\"selected-val-badge\">${cname}</span></td>\n            <td><span class=\"card-tag\">${s.category || 'endpoint'}</span></td>\n            <td>${(s.default_tags || []).map(t => `<span class=\"chip-badge\" style=\"font-size:0.75rem;\">#${t}</span>`).join(' ')}</td>\n            <td style=\"text-align:right;\">\n              <button class=\"btn-action\" style=\"padding:0.25rem 0.5rem; font-size:0.75rem;\" onclick=\"openEditSpecificModal('${s.id}')\">✏ Edit</button>\n              <button class=\"btn-action btn-danger\" style=\"padding:0.25rem 0.5rem; font-size:0.75rem;\" onclick=\"deleteSpecific('${s.id}')\">🗑</button>\n            </td>\n          </tr>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function populateContainerSelect(selectedCid) {\n      const sel = document.getElementById('modal-specific-container');\n      sel.innerHTML = availableContainers.map(c => `\n        <option value=\"${c.id}\" ${c.id === selectedCid ? 'selected' : ''}>${c.name} (${c.type || 'origin'})</option>\n      `).join('');\n    }\n\n    function openCreateSpecificModal(prefillName = \"\") {\n      document.getElementById('specific-modal-title').textContent = \"+ Create Specific Endpoint\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-specific-id').value = generateHexId(\"0003\");\n      document.getElementById('modal-specific-name').value = prefillName;\n      document.getElementById('modal-specific-category').value = \"work\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-specific-tags').value = \"\";\n      populateContainerSelect(availableContainers.length > 0 ? availableContainers[0].id : \"\");\n      openModal('specific-modal');\n    }\n\n    function openEditSpecificModal(id) {\n      const s = availableSpecifics.find(item => item.id === id);\n      if (!s) return;\n      document.getElementById('specific-modal-title').textContent = \"✏ Edit Specific Endpoint\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-specific-id').value = s.id;\n      document.getElementById('modal-specific-name').value = s.name;\n      document.getElementById('modal-specific-category').value = s.category || \"\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-specific-tags').value = (s.default_tags || []).join(', ');\n      populateContainerSelect(s.container_id);\n      openModal('specific-modal');\n    }\n\n    async function saveSpecificModal() {\n      const id = document.getElementById('modal-specific-id').value;\n      const name = document.getElementById('modal-specific-name').value.trim();\n      const container_id = document.getElementById('modal-specific-container').value;\n      const category = document.getElementById('modal-specific-category').value.trim();\n      const tags = document.getElementById('modal-specific-tags').value.split(',').map(t => t.trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean);\n\n      if (!name || !container_id) return alert(\"Name and Parent Container are required.\");\n\n      const payload = { id, name, container_id, category, default_tags: tags };\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/specific`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify(payload)\n      });\n      if (res.ok) {\n        closeModal('specific-modal');\n        fireToast(`Saved Specific: ${name}`);\n        await fetchBackendData();\n        renderSpecificsTable();\n      }\n    }\n\n    async function deleteSpecific(id) {\n      if (!confirm(\"Are you sure you want to delete this specific endpoint?\")) return;\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/specific/delete`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ id })\n      });\n      if (res.ok) {\n        fireToast(\"Specific endpoint deleted\");\n        await fetchBackendData();\n        renderSpecificsTable();\n      }\n    }\n\n    // --- THEMES TREE (VIEW 4) ---\n    function renderThemesTree() {\n      const container = document.getElementById('themes-tree-container');\n      if (availableThemes.length === 0) {\n        container.innerHTML = `<div style=\"padding:1.5rem; text-align:center; color:var(--text-muted);\">No themes yet. Click \"+ Add Root Theme\" to start your tree.</div>`;\n        return;\n      }\n\n      // Render roots and nested children recursively\n      const roots = availableThemes.filter(t => !t.child_of);\n      const childrenMap = {};\n      availableThemes.forEach(t => {\n        if (t.child_of) {\n          if (!childrenMap[t.child_of]) childrenMap[t.child_of] = [];\n          childrenMap[t.child_of].push(t);\n        }\n      });\n\n      function renderNodeHTML(node, level = 0) {\n        const levelClass = level === 1 ? 'child-level-1' : (level >= 2 ? 'child-level-2' : '');\n        const children = childrenMap[node.id] || [];\n        let html = `\n          <div class=\"theme-tree-node ${levelClass}\">\n            <div style=\"display:flex; align-items:center; gap:0.5rem;\">\n              <span style=\"color:var(--accent); font-weight:bold;\">${level === 0 ? '📁' : '↳ 🏷'}</span>\n              <span style=\"font-weight:700; color:var(--text-vivid); font-size:0.92rem;\">${node.name}</span>\n              ${node.notes ? `<span style=\"color:var(--text-muted); font-size:0.78rem; font-style:italic;\">— ${node.notes}</span>` : ''}\n            </div>\n            <div style=\"display:flex; gap:0.35rem;\">\n              <button class=\"btn-action\" style=\"padding:0.2rem 0.45rem; font-size:0.72rem;\" onclick=\"openCreateThemeModal('${node.id}')\">+ Add Child</button>\n              <button class=\"btn-action\" style=\"padding:0.2rem 0.45rem; font-size:0.72rem;\" onclick=\"openEditThemeModal('${node.id}')\">✏</button>\n              <button class=\"btn-action btn-danger\" style=\"padding:0.2rem 0.45rem; font-size:0.72rem;\" onclick=\"deleteThemeNode('${node.id}')\">🗑</button>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        `;\n        children.forEach(child => {\n          html += renderNodeHTML(child, level + 1);\n        });\n        return html;\n      }\n\n      let treeHTML = \"\";\n      roots.forEach(root => {\n        treeHTML += renderNodeHTML(root, 0);\n      });\n      container.innerHTML = treeHTML;\n    }\n\n    function populateParentThemeSelect(selectedParentId) {\n      const sel = document.getElementById('modal-theme-parent');\n      let options = `<option value=\"\">-- Root Level (No Parent) --</option>`;\n      availableThemes.forEach(t => {\n        options += `<option value=\"${t.id}\" ${t.id === selectedParentId ? 'selected' : ''}>${t.name}</option>`;\n      });\n      sel.innerHTML = options;\n    }\n\n    function openCreateThemeModal(parentId = null, prefillName = \"\") {\n      document.getElementById('theme-modal-title').textContent = parentId ? \"+ Add Child Sub-Theme\" : \"+ Add Root Theme\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-theme-id').value = generateHexId(\"0004\");\n      document.getElementById('modal-theme-name').value = prefillName;\n      document.getElementById('modal-theme-notes').value = \"\";\n      populateParentThemeSelect(parentId);\n      openModal('theme-modal');\n    }\n\n    function openEditThemeModal(id) {\n      const t = availableThemes.find(item => item.id === id);\n      if (!t) return;\n      document.getElementById('theme-modal-title').textContent = \"✏ Edit Theme Node\";\n      document.getElementById('modal-theme-id').value = t.id;\n      document.getElementById('modal-theme-name').value = t.name;\n      document.getElementById('modal-theme-notes').value = t.notes || \"\";\n      populateParentThemeSelect(t.child_of);\n      openModal('theme-modal');\n    }\n\n    async function saveThemeModal() {\n      const id = document.getElementById('modal-theme-id').value;\n      const name = document.getElementById('modal-theme-name').value.trim();\n      const child_of = document.getElementById('modal-theme-parent').value || null;\n      const notes = document.getElementById('modal-theme-notes').value.trim();\n\n      if (!name) return alert(\"Theme name is required.\");\n\n      const payload = { id, name, child_of, notes };\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/theme`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify(payload)\n      });\n      if (res.ok) {\n        closeModal('theme-modal');\n        fireToast(`Saved Theme: ${name}`);\n        await fetchBackendData();\n        renderThemesTree();\n      }\n    }\n\n    async function deleteThemeNode(id) {\n      if (!confirm(\"Delete this theme? Any child themes will be unlinked to root level.\")) return;\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/theme/delete`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ id })\n      });\n      if (res.ok) {\n        fireToast(\"Theme deleted\");\n        await fetchBackendData();\n        renderThemesTree();\n      }\n    }\n\n    // --- ARCHIVE (VIEW 5) ---\n    async function loadArchiveList() {\n      try {\n        const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quotes/list`);\n        if (res.ok) {\n          allQuotesList = await res.json();\n          renderArchiveTable();\n        }\n      } catch (e) {}\n    }\n\n    function renderArchiveTable(filtered = null) {\n      const list = filtered || allQuotesList;\n      const tbody = document.getElementById('archive-table-body');\n      if (list.length === 0) {\n        tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan=\"6\" style=\"text-align:center; color:var(--text-muted); padding:1.5rem;\">No quotes found.</td></tr>`;\n        return;\n      }\n      tbody.innerHTML = list.map(q => {\n        const s = availableSpecifics.find(item => item.id === q.specific_id);\n        const sTitle = s ? `📍 ${s.name} · ${s.container ? s.container.name : ''}` : '—';\n        const snippet = q.main_content.split('\\n')[0].substring(0, 48) + '...';\n        return `\n          <tr>\n            <td style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-weight:700; color:var(--accent);\">#${q.index}</td>\n            <td style=\"font-weight:600; color:var(--text-vivid); max-width:320px;\" title=\"${q.main_content}\">“${snippet}”</td>\n            <td><span class=\"selected-val-badge\" style=\"font-size:0.75rem;\">${sTitle}</span></td>\n            <td style=\"font-family:'JetBrains Mono'; font-size:0.8rem;\">${q.date}</td>\n            <td>${(q.tags || []).map(t => `<span class=\"chip-badge\" style=\"font-size:0.72rem;\">#${t}</span>`).join(' ')}</td>\n            <td style=\"text-align:right;\">\n              <button class=\"btn-action\" style=\"padding:0.25rem 0.5rem; font-size:0.75rem;\" onclick=\"loadQuoteForEdit('${q.id}')\">✏ Edit</button>\n              <button class=\"btn-action btn-danger\" style=\"padding:0.25rem 0.5rem; font-size:0.75rem;\" onclick=\"deleteQuoteItem('${q.id}')\">🗑</button>\n            </td>\n          </tr>\n        `;\n      }).join('');\n    }\n\n    function filterArchive(q) {\n      const query = (q || \"\").toLowerCase();\n      const filtered = allQuotesList.filter(item => item.main_content.toLowerCase().includes(query) || item.context.toLowerCase().includes(query));\n      renderArchiveTable(filtered);\n    }\n\n    function loadQuoteForEdit(id) {\n      const q = allQuotesList.find(item => item.id === id);\n      if (!q) return;\n      activeQuote = {\n        id: q.id,\n        index: q.index,\n        main_content: q.main_content,\n        context: q.context || \"\",\n        date: q.date,\n        specific_id: q.specific_id,\n        theme_ids: q.theme_ids || [],\n        usage: q.usage || [],\n        preference: q.preference || [],\n        backlinks: q.backlinks || [],\n        tags: q.tags || [],\n        sentiment: q.sentiment || \"\"\n      };\n      document.getElementById('input-main-content').value = q.main_content;\n      document.getElementById('input-context').value = q.context || \"\";\n      document.getElementById('input-date').value = q.date;\n      document.getElementById('input-backlink').value = (q.backlinks || []).join(', ');\n\n      const s = availableSpecifics.find(item => item.id === q.specific_id);\n      renderSpecificBadge(s);\n      renderThemeChips();\n      renderUsagePills();\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n\n      switchView('quotes-view', document.querySelector('.nav-tab-btn'));\n      fireToast(`Loaded Quote #${q.index} for Editing`);\n    }\n\n    async function deleteQuoteItem(id) {\n      if (!confirm(\"Are you sure you want to delete this quote from SQLite?\")) return;\n      const res = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quote/delete`, {\n        method: 'POST',\n        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ id })\n      });\n      if (res.ok) {\n        fireToast(\"Quote deleted\");\n        await fetchBackendData();\n        await loadArchiveList();\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Modal Helpers\n    function openModal(id) {\n      document.getElementById(id).classList.add('open');\n    }\n\n    function closeModal(id) {\n      document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('open');\n    }\n\n    function toggleTheme() {\n      const html = document.documentElement;\n      const cur = html.getAttribute('data-theme') || 'dark';\n      const nxt = cur === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n      html.setAttribute('data-theme', nxt);\n      localStorage.setItem('prescripts-theme', nxt);\n    }\n\n    // Backend Synchronizer\n    async function fetchBackendData() {\n      try {\n        const taxRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/taxonomy`);\n        if (taxRes.ok) {\n          const tax = await taxRes.json();\n          if (tax.containers) availableContainers = tax.containers;\n          if (tax.specifics) availableSpecifics = tax.specifics;\n          if (tax.themes) availableThemes = tax.themes;\n        }\n\n        const qRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/quotes`);\n        if (qRes.ok) {\n          const quotes = await qRes.json();\n          if (Object.keys(quotes).length > 0) availableQuotes = quotes;\n        }\n\n        const idxRes = await fetch(`${API_BASE}/api/next_index`);\n        if (idxRes.ok) {\n          const data = await idxRes.json();\n          activeQuote.index = data.next_index;\n        }\n      } catch (e) {}\n\n      renderUsagePills();\n      renderPrefChips();\n      renderThemeChips();\n      renderLiveJSON();\n    }\n\n    // Global Click Listener for Dropdowns\n    document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {\n      if (!e.target.closest('#date-host')) {\n        const dd = document.getElementById('date-dropdown');\n        if (dd) dd.classList.remove('open');\n      }\n      if (!e.target.closest('#specific-combo-wrap')) {\n        const menu = document.getElementById('specific-combo-menu');\n        const trigger = document.getElementById('specific-combo-trigger');\n        if (menu) menu.classList.remove('open');\n        if (trigger) trigger.classList.remove('active');\n      }\n      if (!e.target.closest('#theme-host')) {\n        const dd = document.getElementById('theme-dropdown');\n        if (dd) dd.classList.remove('open');\n      }\n      if (!e.target.closest('#pref-host')) {\n        const dd = document.getElementById('pref-dropdown');\n        if (dd) dd.classList.remove('open');\n      }\n    });\n\n    window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {\n OR context LIKE ?\n        ORDER BY idx ASC\n        LIMIT ?;\n    \"\"\", (like_q, like_q, limit))\n    rows = cur.fetchall()\n    results = []\n    for row in rows:\n        results.append({\n            \"id\": row[\"id\"],\n            \"index\": row[\"idx\"],\n            \"main_content\": row[\"main_content\"],\n            \"context\": row[\"context\"] or \"\",\n            \"date\": row[\"date\"],\n            \"specific_id\": row[\"specific_id\"],\n            \"preference\": json.loads(row[\"preference\"]) if row[\"preference\"] else [],\n            \"backlinks\": json.loads(row[\"backlinks\"]) if row[\"backlinks\"] else []\n        })\n    conn.close()\n    return results\n"}

Fetch all specifics (Table 3) pre-joined with containers and default_usage_ids\n    specifics_map = {}\n    specifics_list = []\n    try:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"SELECT id, name, container_id, category, COALESCE(default_usage_ids, '[]') as default_usage_ids, notes FROM specifics ORDER BY name ASC;\")\n        for r in cur.fetchall():\n            cid = r[\"container_id\"]\n            parent_container = containers_map.get(cid, {\"id\": cid, \"name\": cid, \"type\": \"\"})\n            u_ids = json.loads(r[\"default_usage_ids\"]) if r[\"default_usage_ids\"] else []\n            s = {\n                \"id\": r[\"id\"],\n                \"name\": r[\"name\"],\n                \"category\": r[\"category\"] or \"\",\n                \"default_usage_ids\": u_ids,\n                \"notes\": r[\"notes\"] or \"\",\n                \"container\": parent_container\n            }\n            specifics_map[r[\"id\"]] = s\n            specifics_list.append(s)\n    except sqlite3.OperationalError:\n        pass\n\n    # 4. filterActiveTable(q) {\n      const config = TABLES_CONFIG[currentTableKey];\n      const data = config.getData();\n      const query = (q || \"\").toLowerCase().trim();\n\n      if (!query) {\n        renderActiveTable(data);\n        return;\n      }\n\n      const filtered = data.filter(row => {\n        return Object.values(row).some(val => {\n          if (typeof val === 'string') return val.toLowerCase().includes(query);\n          if (Array.isArray(val)) return val.some(item => String(item).toLowerCase().includes(query));\n          return false;\n        });\n      });\n      renderActiveTable(filtered);\n    }\n\n    function openCreateRecordModal() {\n      const config = TABLES_CONFIG[currentTableKey];\n      document.getElementById('generic-modal-title').textContent = function renderGenericModalFields(record, isEdit) {\n      const config = TABLES_CONFIG[currentTableKey];\n      const container = document.getElementById('generic-modal-fields');\n      const recId = record.id || generateHexId(config.prefix);\n\n      container.innerHTML = config.fields.map(f => {\n        const val = record[f.key] !== undefined ? record[f.key] : \"\";\n\n        if (f.type === \"readonly\") {\n          return `\n            <div>\n              <label class=\"field-label\">${f.label}</label>\n              <input type=\"text\" class=\"raw-input-box\" id=\"modal-field-${f.key}\" value=\"${recId}\" readonly style=\"opacity:0.6; font-family:'JetBrains Mono';\">\n            </div>\n          `;\n        }\n\n        if (f.type === \"text\") {\n          return `\n            <div>\n              <label class=\"field-label\">${f.label} ${f.required ? '<span style=\"color:var(--accent);\">*</span>' : ''}</label>\n              <input type=\"text\" class=\"raw-input-box\" id=\"modal-field-${f.key}\" value=\"${val}\">\n            </div>\n          `;\n        }\n\n        if (f.type === \"textarea\") {\n          return `\n            <div>\n              <label class=\"field-label\">${f.label} ${f.required ? '<span style=\"color:var(--accent);\">*</span>' : ''}</label>\n              <textarea class=\"raw-textarea-box\" id=\"modal-field-${f.key}\" style=\"min-height:70px;\">${val}</textarea>\n            </div>\n          `;\n        }\n\n        if (f.type === \"select\") {\n          let optsHTML = \"\";\n          if (f.optionsSource === \"containers\") {\n            optsHTML = availableContainers.map(c => `<option value=\"${c.id}\" ${c.id === val ? 'selected' : ''}>${c.name}</option>`).join('');\n          } else if (f.optionsSource === \"themes_optional\") {\n            optsHTML = `<option value=\"\">-- Root Level (No Parent) --</option>` +\n                       availableThemes.filter(t => t.id !== recId).map(t => `<option value=\"${t.id}\" ${t.id === val ? 'selected' : ''}>${t.name}</option>`).join('');\n          } else if (f.optionsSource === \"specifics\") {\n            optsHTML = availableSpecifics.map(s => `<option value=\"${s.id}\" ${s.id === val ? 'selected' : ''}>${s.name} · ${s.container ? s.container.name : ''}</option>`).join('');\n          }\n          return `\n            <div>\n              <label class=\"field-label\">${f.label}</label>\n              <select class=\"raw-input-box\" id=\"modal-field-${f.key}\" style=\"height:44px;\">\n                ${optsHTML}\n              </select>\n            </div>\n          `;\n        }\n\n        if (f.type === \"multi_select\") {\n          const activeIds = Array.isArray(val) ? val : [];\n          let pills = \"\";\n          if (f.optionsSource === \"usages\") {\n            pills = availableUsages.map(u => {\n              const active = activeIds.includes(u.id);\n              return `\n                <label style=\"display:inline-flex; align-items:center; gap:0.35rem; background:var(--bg-input); padding:0.35rem 0.65rem; border-radius:4px; font-size:0.8rem; cursor:pointer;\">\n                  <input type=\"checkbox\" class=\"modal-multi-chk-${f.key}\" value=\"${u.id}\" ${active ? 'checked' : ''}>\n                  <span>${u.icon || ''} ${u.name}</span>\n                </label>\n              `;\n            }).join('');\n          }\n          return `\n            <div>\n              <label class=\"field-label\">${f.label}</label>\n              <div style=\"display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap; gap:0.4rem; padding:0.5rem; background:var(--bg-card); border:1px solid
